Teaching the deeps of wisdom, the spiritual depths, ye proclaimed the one Godhead of the Trinity, O venerable ones, having described the angelic ranks and the mysteries of their splendors, O God-wise ones; therefore also ordering the lower hierarchy, ye were led unto the one union: O hieromartyrs, pray Christ God to grant remission of sins unto those who honor your holy memory with love. *(Twice)*  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
O cloud of the spiritual Sun, golden-wrought lamp of the divine Light: undefiled, blameless, all-immaculate Sovereign Lady, enlighten my soul darkened by the blindness of passions with the ray of dispassion, I pray thee; and wash my defiled heart with streams of compunction and tears of repentance; and cleanse me from the mire of my deeds, that in love I may cry unto thee: O Mother of God, Ever-Virgin, pray Christ God to grant me remission of transgressions: for in thee do I, thy servant, place my hope.

*After the second Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 5.*  
*Tone 5, Podoben: "The Word co-beginningless" (Собезна́чалное сло́во):*[^342]  
Having catechized your mind with apostolic utterance, and having pastured the assembly of the faithful, O venerable ones, ye offered a pure sacrifice unto the King and Creator Who was incarnate for our sake of the Virgin Mother of God, and arose with glory; Whom ye confessed before tormentors, O hieromartyrs. *(Twice)*  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
Fervent and invincible protectress, sure and unashamed hope, wall and shelter and haven of those who flee unto thee, O pure Ever-Virgin, pray thy Son and God with the angels to grant peace unto the world, and salvation, and great mercy.

*At the Polyeleos, Refrain (Megaplynarion):*[^343]  
We magnify you, O hieromartyrs of Christ, and we honor your holy memory: for ye pray for us unto Christ our God.

*Selected Psalm: Hear these things, all ye nations (Ps. 48:1).*[^344]

*After the Polyeleos, Sessional Hymn, Tone 1.*  
*Tone 1, Podoben: "While the stone was sealed" (Ка́мени запеча́танꙋ):*[^345]  
Having cultivated the fruit of divine knowledge by your labors, ye uprooted godlessness by the networks of teaching; and having anointed yourselves with the oil of grace, ye pastured the people, O hieromartyrs; and having suffered lawfully, ye were vouchsafed double honor from Christ. Glory to Him Who gave you strength! Glory to Him Who crowned you! Glory to Him Who through you works healing for all! *(Twice)*  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
Thy divine hands, wherewith thou didst bear the Creator Who was incarnate in His goodness, stretch forth, O all-pure Virgin, and entreat Him to deliver us from temptations and passions and tribulations, who praise thee in love and cry aloud: Glory to Him Who dwelt in thee! Glory to Him Who came forth from thee! Glory to Him Who delivered us by thy childbearing!

*Songs of Ascents [Anabathmoi], the first Antiphon of Tone 4. Prokeimenon, Tone 4:*[^346]  
Precious in the sight of the Lord is the death of His venerable ones (Ps. 115:6).  
*Verse: What shall I render unto the Lord for all that He hath rendered unto me? (Ps. 115:3).*  
*Let every breath praise the Lord (Ps. 150:6).*  
*Gospel from Luke, Pericope 67 (Luke 12:32–40).*[^347]  
*After Psalm 50, Sticheron, Tone 1. Idiomelon:*[^348]  
As pillars of the Church of Christ, firmly breaking down the walls of unbelief, O fellow-brother martyrs: before your death ye dissolved the heretical faith, praying for our souls.

*If thou celebratest with Polyeleos:*[^349]  
*The Canon of the Mother of God on 4, and of the Hieromartyrs on 8.*  
*Tone 4. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^350]  
Traversing the deep of the Red Sea with dryshod steps, ancient Israel of old conquered the power of Amalek in the wilderness by the cross-stretched hands of Moses.  
*Troparion 1:*  
Enlightened with divine illumination, O glorious ones, by your light-bearing and bright festival which we celebrate, deliver those who piously glorify you from the mist of passions.  
*Troparion 2:*  
The ungodly one was put to shame, O sufferers and servants of Christ the King: for by assailing them, he could not overthrow their strength with his wicked and empty quiver.  
*Troparion 3:*  
Being filled with waters life-creating and spiritual, give drink unto those who are dried up with the burning heat of cruel godlessness, and guide them unto the holy and saving water.  
*Theotokion:*  
The ancestral transgression was loosed by thy childbearing beyond mind, O all-pure one, and the first ones received the entrance into paradise, praising thee with loud voice.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*[^351]  
Thy Church rejoices in Thee, O Christ, crying aloud: Thou art my strength, O Lord, and my refuge and my firmament!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Inflamed spiritually by the fire of the Comforter, the hieromartyrs quenched the deceit of cruel burning, rejoicing.  
*Troparion 2:*  
By the light of the Spirit, O glorious ones, ye drew unto the light of sacred baptism those who were in cruel darkness.  
*Troparion 3:*  
As gold tried by fire, your teachings were shown forth, having the seal of the holy sufferings of martyrdom.  
*Theotokion:*  
Thou didst give birth unto the strong God Who delivers mankind from the violence of the enemy, O all-pure, all-immaculate Virgin.

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 3.*  
*Tone 3, Podoben: "Of divine faith" (Бж҃е́ственныѧ вѣ́ры):*[^352]  
Having been enriched with the chrism of anointing, ye were shown forth as pastors unto God-wise people; and as pure lambs ye sacrificed and offered yourselves unto the Word Who was first sacrificed as a sheep, O all-praised martyrs and universal luminaries: therefore with love we all celebrate your divine memory.  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
Our refuge and strength art thou, O Mother of God, sovereign help of the world; by thy prayers shelter thy servants from all necessity, O only blessed one.  
*Cross-Theotokion:*[^353]  
Having acquired a rod of power, the Cross of thy Son, O Mother of God, thereby do we cast down the insolence of enemies, unceasingly magnifying thee in love.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*[^354]  
Beholding Thee, the Sun of righteousness, lifted up upon the Cross, the Church stood in its place, crying out as is meet: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!  
*Troparion 1:*  
The choir of sacred martyrs sings praise unto Thee, adorned with the grace of the hierarchal office and with martyrdom, crying aloud in gladness: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Being instructed in divine mysteries, O God-bearing fathers, ye pastured the people in holiness and righteousness, and received a divine end through martyrdom.  
*Troparion 3:*  
Adorned with apostolic and prophetic divine gifts, the divine choir of hieromartyrs cries aloud in gladness: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!  
*Theotokion:*  
Catechized by God-proclaiming utterances, O Mother of God, beholding the fulfillment of what was spoken of thee, we cry aloud: Rejoice, O all-immaculate Virgin, who by thy childbearing hast brought us deliverance!

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*[^355]  
Thou, my Lord, camest as a light into the world, a holy light turning those from dark ignorance who sing praise unto Thee in faith.  
*Troparion 1:*  
With the divine rays of most precious preaching, ye shone forth a grace-filled light upon those who sit in the darkness of deceit.  
*Troparion 2:*  
Now beholding Christ face to face, ye enjoy ineffable Light: pray Him for us!  
*Troparion 3:*  
The divine river of Christ's teachings was filled in you; stop up the streams of deceit, and firmly nourish the minds of the faithful.  
*Theotokion:*  
The Lord chose thee alone out of all generations, O Mother of God; incarnate of thee, He deified humanity.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*[^356]  
I will sacrifice unto Thee with a voice of praise, O Lord, the Church cries unto Thee, cleansed from the gore of demons by the blood that for mercy's sake flowed from Thy ribs.  
*Troparion 1:*  
Having lived in courage and wisdom, O Lord, the choir of Thy saints now rejoices in Thee, in righteousness, prayer, and chastity.  
*Troparion 2:*  
Strengthened by the hope of eternal life, the valiant ones rejoiced as they endured bodily tearings, sacred zealots of the divine prophets!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Nurtured by spiritual laws, O most wise ones, ye offered saving laws unto the lawless, and brought them unto the light of the right faith.  
*Theotokion:*  
Adam was stripped of life unto deadness in paradise; but through thy childbearing, O pure Bride of God, he was made immortal by the life-creating One.

*Kontakion in the Typikon.*[^357]

*If there is no Typikon, speak this Kontakion, Tone 3:*[^358]  
*Tone 3, Podoben: "Today the Virgin" (Дѣ́ва дне́сь):*[^359]  
As never-setting luminaries of the Spiritual Sun, coming together today let us praise you in songs; for ye shone forth unto those who were in the darkness of ignorance, summoning all unto the height of piety, O hieromartyrs. Therefore we cry unto you: Rejoice, foundation of all ascetics!  
*Ikos:*  
Shine upon me, O Jesus, Giver of life, Who in the beginning didst form divine beauty, that I may hymn Thy saints who struggled for Thee even unto death, and cast down the strength of the enemy, and refuting abominable godlessness, were established by the power of the Cross, O Christ, and worthily received glory from Thee: therefore we cry unto them: Rejoice, foundation of all ascetics!

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*[^360]  
In the Persian furnace the children of Abraham, burned rather by love of piety than by the flame, cried aloud: Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy glory, O Lord!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Enlightened with the chrism of the hierarchy, O God-wise ones, and made divinely comely on both sides with martyric blood, ye cry aloud: Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy glory, O Lord!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Directing earthly thoughts as in the twinkling of an eye, as in a ship of all-pure glory, O all-praised ones, being elevated, ye cry aloud: Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy glory, O Lord!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Without knowing the opposite intention, ye put to shame every tormenting deceitful knowledge, O glorious hieromartyrs, singing: Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy glory, O Lord!  
*Theotokion:*  
He Who covered the earth with mist and the heaven with clouds, O Lord, clothed Himself in mortal flesh from thy blood, O Virgin, and clothed men in the garment of the glory of immortality.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*[^361]  
Daniel, stretching out his hands, stopped the gaping mouths of lions in the pit; and the youths, zealots of piety, girded with virtue, quenched the power of the fire, crying aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Beaten with sinews, and with fleshly wounds, ye obtained incorruptible life, O hieromartyrs, and delivered all who were serving dead idols; who, being saved, cry aloud with you unceasingly: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!  
*Troparion 2:*  
As clusters of the spiritual vineyard, O hieromartyrs, ye now pour forth the wine of healing, and gladden the hearts of all who praise you in faith, and cry aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!  
*Troparion 3:*  
The tombs of the hieromartyrs poured forth wonders upon the universe, crushing the deceitful apparitions of the evil demon, and granting abundant healings unto those who cry aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!  
*Theotokion:*  
He Who was seen as a man, yet was before all being, was numbered with double essence, O all-pure one; without alteration of actions or wills, He was made like us in His will, and appeared: Whom we sing in faith: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*[^362]  
A cornerstone was cut without hands from thee, O Virgin, from that unhewn mountain, even Christ, Who joined together the separated natures: therefore rejoicing, we magnify thee, O Mother of God!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Celebrating the sacred memory of the holy hieromartyrs, come, let us all honor them with loud voice in sacred songs, as they pray for us unto our all-merciful God.

*Troparion 2:*  
Being comforted by the Word, having cleansed all defilements of your souls, and having put off the garments of skin by torments, ye clothed yourselves in the most fair robe of salvation.  
*Troparion 3:*  
Gathering in memory of the holy passion-bearers, O ye people, to receive grace and sing praise unto the valiant hieromartyrs, who suffered manfully and cast down the author of evil.  
*Theotokion:*  
Bearing in thy hands Christ Who by His nod bears all things: pray Him as thy Son, O all-pure one, to deliver me from the assaults of enemies, and to encompass me with the abyss of mercy, and to save me.

*Exapostilarion:*  
*Tone 2, Podoben: "Give ear, ye women" (Жены̀ ᲂу҆слы́шите):*[^363]  
Now a notable day hath appeared unto us, possessing the sacred successors of the sacred apostles: both the God-bearing preachers of the faith together, and the invincible martyrs, whose supplicatory memory we all faithfully celebrate.  
*Theotokion:*  
Thou didst give birth unto the Angel of Great Counsel of the Father, even Christ the King of glory, O all-hymned Maiden; Whose Cross the hieromartyrs proclaimed, and enlightened the nations, and taught them to glorify thee as Mother of God, and to bow down before thy childbearing.

*If thou celebratest with Polyeleos: At the Praises, Stichera, Tone 4.*  
*Tone 4, Podoben: "As one valiant among martyrs" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́бла):*[^364]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
With sacred anointing and with martyric blood, the all-glorious hieromartyrs drew near unto God: natural flower, spiritual beauty, summits of wisdom, correctness of theology, rule of faith, all-constituted rectification, and splendor of the churches. *(Twice)*  
*Sticheron 2:*  
Having stirred up glorification for the suffering and magnification for the martyrs, be strengthened, O God-wise ones, to dare valiantly against diverse torments, and bonds, and prisons, and strippings of bodies, and scourgings, and cruel cold, and finally wounds and death, O hieromartyrs!  
*Sticheron 3:*  
Because of manifold tortures, having put off mortification, O glorious martyrs, ye clothed yourselves in the robe of incorruption, and now dwell in the heavens, ever standing before the divine throne: therefore in faith we celebrate your all-radiant memory, kissing the shrine of your relics.  
*Glory... Tone 8:*  
Among hierarchs and martyrs ye excelled, O venerable ones, and were shown forth as faithful pastors, having drunk the cup of Christ: therefore, having well-pleased Christ in both, pray Him for all of us, as ye now make your abode in the light with the servants on high.  
*Both now... Theotokion:*  
Having taken up the voice of the Archangel Gabriel, let us say: Rejoice, O Mother of God, who didst give birth unto Christ the Life-giver for the world!

*At the Liturgy, the Beatitudes from the Canon, Ode 3 and Ode 6. Prokeimenon, Tone 7:*[^365]  
Precious in the sight of the Lord is the death of His venerable ones (Ps. 115:6).  
*Verse: What shall I render unto the Lord for all that He hath rendered unto me? (Ps. 115:3).*  
*Epistle to the Hebrews, Pericope 334 from the midpoint (Heb. 13:7–16). Or to the Philippians, Pericope 247 (Phil. 3:20–4:3). Alleluia, Tone 2:*[^366]  
*Verse: Thy priests shall be clothed with righteousness (Ps. 131:9).*  
*Gospel from Luke, Pericope 104 (Luke 21:12–19). Or 52 (Luke 10:16–21). Or 77 (Luke 14:25–35).*[^367]  
*Communion Hymn: Rejoice in the Lord, O ye venerable ones (Ps. 32:1).*[^368]

## Common Service to a Single Venerable-Martyr

*At Vespers, on "Lord, I have cried", Stichera, Tone 6.*  
*Tone 6, Podoben: "Having risen on the third day" (Трїдне́венъ):*[^369]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
The exploits of the wise faster and the sufferings of the soldier of Christ, we the faithful fittingly praising, let us cry unto the Lord: By His prayers, O Christ God, deliver us from every distress.  
*Sticheron 2:*  
Much peace shall be given thee by God, O most valiant one, when thou hast passed through the tempest of cruel things, O venerable father [Name], invincible soldier and intercessor for those who praise thee.  
*Sticheron 3:*  
Thou didst suffer lawfully, O wise one, and wast all-wise toward God, as another home for the Creator of all things, O martyr; and thou wast shown forth, O God-bearer, as a luminary of the Church, an embellishment of the venerable.  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
O pure Virgin Mother of God, pray unto the Lord that by thy prayers He may grant unto our souls remission of sins, peace, and great mercy.  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
Beholding our Life hanging upon the Tree, the all-immaculate Mother of God, weeping maternally, cried aloud: O my Son and my God, save those who in love sing praise unto Thee!  
*If thou celebratest with Polyeleos: On "Lord, I have cried", Stichera on 6. Glory... Tone 5:*  
O venerable father, thou didst not give sleep unto thine eyes, nor slumber unto thine eyelids, until thou hadst freed soul and body from passions, and hadst prepared thyself as a dwelling of the Spirit; for Christ, coming with the Father, made His abode in thee: having received grace of the consubstantial Trinity, O great preacher, venerable one, pray for our souls!  
*Both now... Theotokion:*  
*In the Red Sea:*[^370]  
*Entrance. Prokeimenon of the day. And three readings:*  
*See in the Service to a Martyr (see fol. 45).[^371]*  
*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 1. Podoben: "O all-lauded martyrs" (Всехва́льніи мч҃нцы):*[^372]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
O all-lauded [Name], thou didst commit thy life to the care of virtues; for with hymnody the faithful in faith praise Christ, obeying thy teachings: standing before the divine Spirit, pray Him that they may be established in peace.  
*Verse: Precious in the sight of the Lord is the death of His venerable ones (Ps. 115:6).*  
*Sticheron 2:*  
O God-inspired martyr [Name], thy life indeed was equal to the angels, an adornment to earthly ones; and to the heavenly ones, as a sweet-smelling sacrifice and good incense was thine acknowledgment offered; and now pray that peace and great mercy be granted unto our souls.  
*Verse: Blessed is the man that fears the Lord, he shall delight exceedingly in His commandments (Ps. 111:1).*  
*Sticheron 3:*  
O most wealthy [Name], having become a vanquisher of passions, thou didst vanquish the tormentor; rejoicing together with the crowned ones, O blessed one, remember all who celebrate thy memory; and now pray Christ that peace and great mercy be granted unto our souls.  
*Glory... Tone 4:*  
In spiritual likeness, O venerable-martyr [Name], having conquered the opposing enemy as an invincible soldier, with the weapon of faith thou didst cut down the Ethiopians of mental darkness; and having fulfilled the law of thy struggles, thou wast crowned with all the choirs of the passion-bearers, O venerable and most wealthy one!

*Both now... Theotokion:*  
*Look upon the supplications of thy servants:*  
*If thou dost not celebrate, speak this Theotokion:*  
Deliver us from our necessities, O Mother of Christ God, who didst give birth unto the Creator of all: that we all may cry unto thee: Rejoice, only protection of our souls!  
*Cross-Theotokion. Tone 4, Podoben: "As one valiant among martyrs" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́бла):*[^373]  
Beholding Christ the Lover of mankind crucified and pierced in the ribs with a spear, the all-pure one wept, crying aloud: What is this, O my Son? What have ungrateful people rendered unto Thee instead of the good things which Thou didst for them? And art Thou in haste to leave me childless, O most beloved One? I marvel, O compassionate One, at Thy voluntary crucifixion!  
*Troparion in the Typikon.*  
*If there is no Typikon, speak this Troparion, Tone 8:*[^374]  
In thee, O father, was preserved with certainty that which is according to the image; for taking up the cross, thou didst follow Christ, and in deed didst teach to disregard the flesh, for it passes away, but to care for the soul, an immortal thing: therefore together with the angels thy spirit rejoices, O venerable [Name]!  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion according to the Tone of the Troparion.*  
*At Matins, on "God is the Lord", the same Troparion.*  
*After the 1st Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 4. Podoben: "Thou Who wast lifted up upon the Cross" (Вознесы́йся на крⷭ҇тъ во́лею):*[^375]  
Thou didst not regard the enjoyment of earthly and corruptible things, and didst love life in the desert, and hated the beauties of the world and fleeting food; and thence wast thou vouchsafed to be joined unto the choir of martyrs and ascetics: with them pray to save thy servants. *(Twice)*  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
He Who sits upon the cherubic throne and makes His abode in the bosom of the Father, sits upon His holy throne in thy womb, O Lady; for God, having become incarnate, truly reigned over all nations: and with understanding now we sing unto Him: Whom pray to save thy servants.  
*After the 2nd Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 8. Podoben: "Of wisdom" (Премꙋ́дрости):*[^376]  
Imitating the manner of John the Baptist and the virtues of Elijah the Tishbite by fasting, thou didst live bodilessly, glorifying the holy divine Trinity with the angels, and conquering demonic assaults with manliness; therefore also by suffering thou didst struggle valiantly, bowing down before Christ's divine incarnation with the Godhead, O blessed [Name]: pray Him to grant remission of sins unto those who in love celebrate thy holy memory. *(Twice)*  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
As a Virgin and the only one among women, who without seed gavest birth unto God in the flesh, all we generations of men call thee blessed; for the fire of the Godhead made its abode in thee, and thou sucklest as an infant the Creator and Lord: therefore we, the angelic and human race, worthily glorify thy most holy childbearing, and with one accord cry unto thee: Pray Christ God to grant remission of sins unto those who worthily sing thy glory.  
*At the Polyeleos, Megalynarion:*[^377]  
We magnify thee, O venerable-martyr [Name], and we honor thy holy memory, instructor of monks and companion of angels!  
*Selected Psalm: With patience I waited for the Lord (Ps. 39:1).*[^378]  
*After the Polyeleos, Sessional Hymn, Tone 4. Podoben: "Thou hast appeared today" (Ꙗ҆ви́лся є҆сѝ):*[^379]  
Having been proven in fasting, thou didst blossom forth in suffering, and enlightened the faithful with thy struggles, O blessed [Name]: therefore we all with one accord magnify thee.

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
O unashamed Hope of those who hope in thee, who alone above nature gavest birth to Christ our God in the flesh: pray Him together with the holy venerable-martyrs to grant unto the universe cleansing of transgressions, and unto all of us amendment of life before the end.

*Songs of Ascents, 1st Antiphon of Tone 4. Prokeimenon:*[^380]  
Precious in the sight of the Lord is the death of His venerable ones (Ps. 115:6).[^381]  
*Verse: What shall I render unto the Lord for all that He hath rendered unto me? (Ps. 115:3).*  
Let every breath praise the Lord.  
*Gospel from Mark, Pericope 37 (Mark 8:34–9:1).*[^382]  
*After Psalm 50, Sticheron, Tone 6:*[^383]  
Having preserved that which is according to the image unharmed, O venerable father [Name], in that which is according to Christ's faith thou didst resist patiently, not fearing the tormentor's threat, but with the spiritual sword didst slay him: therefore having acquired boldness toward God, save from all distresses those who honor thee, O venerable sufferer!

*If thou celebratest with Polyeleos.*  
*Canon of the Theotokos on 4, and of the Venerable-Martyr on 8.*[^384]  
*Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^385]  
Traversing the deep on foot as on dry land, Israel, beholding the pursuer Pharaoh drowned, cried aloud: Let us sing a song of victory unto God!  
*Troparion 1:*  
From worldly temptations and from passions that distress me, calm my mind, O venerable sufferer, and vouchsafe me to keep thy memory in quietness unto thy praise.  
*Troparion 2:*  
Having received the crowns of suffering as a sufferer, O venerable [Name], thou wast translated from earth unto a peaceful world, and unto true light and life, O most blessed one.  
*Troparion 3:*  
Having quenched the tormentor's flame with the streams of thy blood, O pious martyr, thou didst water the souls of the faithful, causing the desire of eternal life to sprout forth, O venerable one.  
*Theotokion:*  
Having given birth unto the uncontainable Word contained in thy womb, O tabernacle containing God: free me from the cruel tempests of evils that oppress me, O pure one!

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*[^386]  
There is none holy like Thee, O Lord my God, Who hast exalted the horn of Thy faithful, O Good One, and hast established us upon the rock of Thy confession.  
*Troparion 1:*  
Thou wast strengthened by the power of God, stepping forth unto struggles with boldness, O most blessed one, enduring diseases and tormenting sufferings, O passion-bearer, our venerable father!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Christ the great Bestower of the contest gave thee strength to crush deceit and put to shame the enemy who boasts shamelessly and rages in vain.  
*Troparion 3:*  
Thy fair feet were shown forth, O venerable one, brightly entering upon the path of martyrdom and crushing the heads of the wicked, O much-suffering [Name]!  
*Theotokion:*  
Her whom Jacob of old saw stretched from earth unto heavenly height, clearly the divine ladder, Mary the pure palace of the Master, let us hymn.

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4.*  
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Joseph was amazed" (Оу҆диви́ся І҆ѡ́сифъ):*[^387]  
Thou wast baptized thrice, with three baptisms faithfully: with water, and with fire, and with the divine Spirit: first with tears; secondly, having struggled firmly with many abstinences, living an angel-like life upon earth, O blessed [Name]; thirdly, as an acceptable lamb thou wast sacrificed unto Christ our God: pray Him unceasingly that our souls may be saved!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*[^388]  
Joseph was amazed, beholding that which was beyond nature, and called to mind the rain upon the fleece in thy seedless conception, O Mother of God, the bush unburned by fire, the rod of Aaron that budded; and thy betrothed and guardian, bearing witness, cried unto the priests: A Virgin gives birth, and after childbirth remains still a Virgin!  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
Thoughts from many hearts are revealed, wounding my mind with the spear of Thy sufferings in Thy voluntary crucifixion: O Child, whoever beholds my weeping and the tearing of my flesh shall know Thee to be the most true God: O Word, I hymn Thy compassion, for the sake of which Thou endurest crucifixion!

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*[^389]  
Christ is my power, God and Lord, the venerable Church sings in a manner becoming God, crying out from a pure mind, feasting in the Lord.  
*Troparion 1:*  
Lawfully didst thou fast, lawfully didst thou suffer, and lawfully wast thou crowned, being strengthened by the Word of God, and keeping thyself unharmed, O most blessed one.  
*Troparion 2:*  
Not bearing to follow deceit, O venerable one, unto the knowledge of God as one prudent Thou didst joyfully draw near: having received spiritual light, Thou wast shown forth as a luminary.  
*Troparion 3:*  
By sacred rite, as a holy voluntary slaughter, thou wast brought, O [Name], unto Him Who was sacrificed for thy sake, and in the temple as a first-born thou didst rest, rejoicing.  
*Theotokion:*  
Thou didst remain a Virgin after childbirth, even as thou wast before birth: for thou gavest birth unto God the Word, Who delivered us from corruption through thy mediation, O all-immaculate one.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*[^390]  
With Thy divine light, O Good One, enlighten in love, I pray, the souls of those who rise early unto Thee, that they may know Thee, O Word of God, to be the true God, calling them from the darkness of sins.  
*Troparion 1:*  
The enemy was wounded by the weapon of the great endurance of the valiant-minded martyr; and he was trampled under his feet, and was shown forth as a laughing-stock, being put to shame.  
*Troparion 2:*  
Having put to silence the malice of the tormentor, thou wast shown forth gentle and meek, O all-honored martyr; by thy bloody end thou wast entwined with an incorruptible crown.  
*Troparion 3:*  
With martyric crowns and ascetic labors our only God, the Rewarder of labors, Who strengthened thee against the murderer, truly honored and crowned thee, O venerable [Name]!  
*Theotokion:*  
Understanding from afar mysteriously, the prophet-like God-beholding choir, O Maiden, Bride of God, foresignified thee in sacred images, perceiving the ineffable depth of thy divine childbearing.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*[^391]  
Beholding the sea of life surging with the tempest of temptations, fleeing unto Thy calm haven I cry unto Thee: Lead up my life from corruption, O most Merciful One!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Thou didst sit upon the waters, steered by the Life-originating hand, O our father, and wast preserved, wherein the persecutor's decrees were drowned through thine earnest prayer, O sacred one.  
*Troparion 2:*  
Exchanging the small for the great, thou didst deliver thyself unto torments, O venerable one; by the Spirit thou didst slay the serpent that deceived Eve, O God-revealed one.  
*Troparion 3:*  
The inventor of malice could not endure the power of thy words: therefore the cruel deceiver delivered thee, O [Name], unto bonds and wounds and cruel death.  
*Theotokion:*  
Lo, now of thy pure blood, O Virgin Mother, the Lord took flesh, and unites Himself unto men beyond custom, for the sake of ineffable mercy working our salvation.

*Kontakion in the Typikon.*[^392]  
*If there is no Typikon, speak this Kontakion, Tone 2:*[^393]  
*Tone 2, Podoben: "The firm" (Тве́рдыѧ):*[^394]  
As a pious and proven faster, and an honored sufferer by free choice, and a conformable dweller of the desert, in hymns let us worthily praise [Name] ever-praised: for he trampled the serpent!  
*Ikos:*  
Emulating the sufferings of the Lord, O most blessed one, thou didst desire to die for Him; thou didst destroy the deceit of idolatrous priesthood; manfully having struggled against the enemies, thou didst cast down their malicious snares and craftiness, and received the crown of honor, O [Name], venerable sufferer: therefore in faith we flee unto thy shelter, and cry unto thee: Deliver us from visible and invisible enemies, for thou hast trampled the serpent!

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*[^395]  
The angel made the furnace drop dew for the venerable youths, but the burning command of God consumed the Chaldeans, persuading the tormentor to cry out: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Having turned thy mind away from affection toward the flesh, O blessed one, being burned with fire and consumed, O wise one, thou didst sing the song of the youths: Blessed is the God of our fathers!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Driven away by thy calling alone are the evil spirits, who recognized thee as an ascetic and a martyr of Christ, O venerable one, singing warmly: Blessed is the God of our fathers!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Thy demise was revealed unto thee, O most blessed [Name], by the all-working will: therefore thou didst give thyself unto greater fasting, and passing from glory to glory, thou wast slain for the sake of Christ, the God over all.  
*Theotokion:*  
Raise me up who am held by despondency, O all-pure one, unto the doing of God's deeds, strengthening me against the enemies who ever cruelly fight against me and entice me with contrary thoughts.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*[^396]  
From the flame Thou didst pour forth dew for the venerable ones, and with water didst burn the sacrifice of the righteous one; for Thou dost all things, O Christ, only by willing: Thee do we exalt unto all ages!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Adorned with ascetic beauties and worn out by the pain of wounds, thou wast shown forth unto the Bestower of victory, O all-glorious one, having received from Him the trophies of victory.  
*Troparion 2:*  
Being inflamed with love for the Ruler of all, O [Name] all-glorious, thou didst cast down the enemy's haughty neck; and by thy desire toward God, thou didst receive Him dwelling in thee unto ages.  
*Troparion 3:*  
Vouchsafe me the mercy of God, O God-blessed martyr, and deliver from temptations and distresses him who honors thee, O [Name], venerable one, and flees under thy shelter.  
*Theotokion:*  
Rejoice, O holy mountain traversed by God; rejoice, manifestation of sacred mysteries and fearful report and visible sight of the heavens; rejoice, O Virgin, raising up of the fallen!

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*[^397]  
It is not possible for men to see God, upon Whom the ranks of angels dare not gaze; but through thee, O all-pure one, the Word incarnate was shown forth unto men: Whom magnifying, with the heavenly hosts we call thee blessed.  
*Troparion 1:*  
Thou hast attained unto the ultimate beauty and sweetness, O our God-wise father [Name], and wast near unto God, singing with the bodiless hosts: Holy, holy, holy art Thou, O Trinity all-working and all-powerful!

*Troparion 2:*  
Having escaped from the nets of those who hunt thee, thou didst rest in heavenly beauty, O venerable [Name], and wast united with the armies of martyrs: with the bodiless hosts now pray for all of us, that we may call thee blessed.  
*Troparion 3:*  
Having made Christ thy strength and dominion, thou didst drive away the malice of demons by prayer, and didst reach the havens on high, entirely adorned, richly illuminated with divine effusions of light.  
*Theotokion:*  
Not being able to comprehend the incomprehensible wonder of thy childbearing, O Virgin Mother, glorifying it rather in silence, we sing praise unto thee, the most blessed, and only fair and all-immaculate one among women.

*Exapostilarion:*  
*Tone 2, Podoben: "Give ear, ye women" (Жены̀ ᲂу҆слы́шите):*[^398]  
First being well proven by fasting, and secondly having finished the divine course by suffering, thou didst ascend unto the heavens and standest before Christ, O [Name], venerable father: pray warmly for us who sing praise unto thee.  
*Theotokion:*  
Having given birth unto ineffable Joy, the Lord: make us partakers of heavenly joy, all who honor thee, O all-pure one, the true grace, crying unto thee from the soul: Forget not thy servants, O blessed Mary!

*If thou celebratest with Polyeleos: At the Praises, Stichera, Tone 6.*  
*Sticheron 1:*  
Having crimsoned the sacred robe with martyric blood, O venerable one, thou didst enter into the Holy of Holies worthily, O glorious one, where is ineffable light, where is divine glory, where is the voice of those who keep festival; from Whom for thy labors thou didst receive the martyric victories, and the unfading crown, and eternal good glory, and paradise life, O most wise [Name]: with boldness pray for our souls! *(Twice)*  
*Sticheron 2:*  
The tempest of torments did not shake thy soul, nor the confinement of long duration in the dark prison concealing thee, O glorious sufferer; but as a luminary thou didst shine forth light unto those suffering in darkness, whom thou didst show forth as sons of the day by the laver of baptism, by the greater and saving begetting, and by divine dwelling: as a God-accepted sufferer, O most wise [Name]!  
*Sticheron 3:*  
Who shall worthily declare the victories of thy virtues? What mouth shall utter thy torments and endurance? For in both thou didst well-please Christ, O [Name]: and now cease not praying, O favored one, to save our souls, as having great boldness, O venerable-martyr!  
*Glory... Tone 2:*  
Come, let us crown the sufferer of Christ with songs, as a lily of the field, and a fair-blooming flower of the divine paradise: father of the Orthodox, beauty of purity, establishment of the faith, glory of abstinence was he clearly shown forth unto the world: therefore he received an immutable crown of the kingdom.  
*Both now... Theotokion:*  
All my hope I place upon thee, O Mother of God: keep me under thy shelter.

*At the Liturgy, the Beatitudes from the Canon, Ode 3 and Ode 6. Prokeimenon, Tone 7:*[^399]  
The venerable ones shall boast in glory, and they shall rejoice upon their beds (Ps. 149:5).  
*Verse: Sing unto the Lord a new song, His praise in the church of the venerable ones (Ps. 149:1).*  
*Epistle to Timothy, Pericope 291 (2 Tim. 2:1–10). Alleluia, Tone 6:*[^400]  
Blessed is the man that fears the Lord, he shall delight exceedingly in His commandments (Ps. 111:1).  
*Verse: His seed shall be mighty upon the earth (Ps. 111:2).*  
*Gospel from Mark, Pericope 37 (Mark 8:34–9:1).*[^401]  
*Communion Hymn: In everlasting memory shall the righteous be (Ps. 111:6b).*[^402]

## Common Service to Two and Many Venerable-Martyrs

*At Vespers, on "Lord, I have cried", Stichera, Tone 8.*  
*Tone 8, Podoben: "What shall we call you, O holy ones" (Что̀ вы̀ нарече́мъ):*[^403]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
Having struggled greatly, O venerable ones, ye endured the tormentor's assaults valiantly: ye gave up your souls unto the sword with love, ye are clothed in martyric crowns: ye dance worthily with the angels; many were your endurances, and greater the gifts: pray for our souls!  
*Sticheron 2:*  
Ye lived well, O holy ones, having become like unto martyric struggles, O valiant ones; for ye manfully disregarded torments, by fasting ye cleansed your bodies, and in love ye poured forth your blood: that together ye might be clothed in the crowns of your sufferings; pray that our souls may be saved!  
*Sticheron 3:*  
O venerable martyrs of Christ, ye struggled well, having disregarded temporary life, ye manfully and voluntarily trampled fleshly wisdom; and thus having finished your course in Christ, ye make your abode worthily with the angels: therefore honoring your memory we pray: Pray the Lord that our souls may be shown mercy!  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
Unto whom hast thou become like, O wretched soul, not rising at all unto repentance, and not fearing the fire awaiting the wicked? Arise, and call upon the only speedy one unto protection, and cry aloud: O Virgin Mother, pray thy Son and our God to deliver me from the net of the deceiver!  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
When the Ewe-lamb saw the Lamb stretched voluntarily upon the Tree of the Cross, weeping maternally, suffering with tears she cried aloud: O My Son, what is this strange sight? Thou Who givest life unto all as Lord, how art Thou put to death, O longsuffering One, granting resurrection unto earthly ones? I glorify Thy great condescension, O My God!  
*If thou celebratest with Polyeleos: On "Lord, I have cried", Stichera on 6. Glory... Tone 8:*  
Having lived an angel-like life, by ascetic pains, O venerable fathers, having enslaved the bodies by abstinence, ye subjected them unto spiritual things, having become doers of the commandments of the Lord: from the beginning ye preserved that which is according to the image, and finished the struggles of suffering from ascetic sweats and martyric torments, and were adorned with double crowns: earnestly pray the Savior that we may be saved!  
*Both now... Theotokion:*  
*The King of heaven for His love of mankind appeared on earth:*[^404]  
*Entrance. Prokeimenon of the day. And three readings:*  
*See in the Service to Martyrs (see fol. 54v).[^405]*  
*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 8. Podoben: "Martyrs of the Lord" (Мч҃нцы твоѝ Гдⷭ҇и):*[^406]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
Thy venerable martyrs, O Lord, imitating the bodiless hosts, by prayers and abstinence drove away fleshly passions; and blossomed forth with wonders, and enlightened the hearts of all: by their supplication grant Thy people great mercy!  
*Verse: Precious in the sight of the Lord is the death of His venerable ones (Ps. 115:6).*  
*Sticheron 2:*  
The venerable martyrs, having received virtuous zeal, openly destroyed the heresy of Arius and of Nestorius, and having become champions for the Orthodox, received glory from all: by their prayers, O Christ, grant Thy people great mercy!

*Verse: Blessed is the man that fears the Lord, he shall delight exceedingly in His commandments (Ps. 111:1).*  
*Sticheron 3:*  
Having appeared as stars of the firmament of abstinence, ye enlightened the souls of those who lead the monastic life, and drove away the darkness of demons, O venerable martyrs: therefore after your demise ye are called blessed, praying that all who perform your holy memory may be saved!  
*Glory... Tone 6:*  
Blessed are ye, O venerable martyrs of Christ our God: venerable indeed, for ye loved righteousness, and received grace; and martyrs, for the sword did not separate you from the love of Christ: and for this cause ye rejoice, for your reward is great in the heavens!  
*Both now... Theotokion:*  
*My Maker and Redeemer:*  
*If thou dost not celebrate, speak this Theotokion:*  
No one who flees unto thee departs from thee ashamed, O all-pure Virgin Mother of God: but he asks for grace, and receives a gift according to his profitable request.  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
Beholding Thee crucified, O Christ, she who gave Thee birth cried aloud: What is this strange mystery that I behold, O My Son? How dost Thou die upon the Tree, suspended in the flesh, O Giver of life?  
*Troparion in the Typikon.*  
*If there is no Typikon, speak this Troparion, Tone 4:*[^407]  
O God of our fathers, Who dealest ever with us according to Thy meekness, take not Thy mercy away from us, but by their prayers direct our life in peace!  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion according to the Tone of the Troparion.*  
*At Matins, on "God is the Lord", the same Troparion.*  
*After the 1st Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 4. Podoben: "Thou Who wast lifted up upon the Cross" (Вознесы́йся на крⷭ҇тъ):*[^408]  
Ye disregarded the enjoyment of earthly and corruptible things, and loved life in the desert, having hated the beauties of the world and fleeting food; and thence were ye vouchsafed to be joined unto the choir of martyrs and ascetics: with them pray to save your servants. *(Twice)*  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
He Who sits upon the cherubic throne and makes His abode in the bosom of the Father, sits upon His holy throne in thy womb, O Lady; for God, having become incarnate, truly reigned over all nations: and with understanding now we sing unto Him: Whom pray to save thy servants.  
*After the 2nd Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 8. Podoben: "Of wisdom" (Премꙋ́дрости):*[^409]  
Imitating the manner of John the Baptist and the virtues of Elijah the Tishbite by fasting, ye lived bodilessly, glorifying the holy divine Trinity with the angels, and conquered demonic assaults with manliness; therefore also in sufferings ye struggled firmly, bowing down before Christ's divine incarnation with the Godhead, O blessed venerable-martyrs: pray Christ God to grant remission of sins unto those who in love celebrate your holy memory. *(Twice)*  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
As a Virgin and the only one among women, who without seed gavest birth unto God in the flesh, all we generations of men call thee blessed; for the fire of the Godhead made its abode in thee, and thou sucklest as an infant the Creator and Lord: therefore we, the angelic and human race, worthily glorify thy most holy childbearing, and with one accord cry unto thee: Pray Christ God to grant remission of sins unto those who worthily sing thy glory.
*At the Polyeleos, Megalynarion:*  
We magnify you, O venerable martyrs, and we honor your holy memory, instructors of monks and companions of angels!  
*Selected Psalm: With patience I waited for the Lord (Ps. 39:1).*[^410]  
*After the Polyeleos, Sessional Hymn, Tone 8. Podoben: "Of wisdom" (Премꙋ́дрости):*  
Brightly illuminated by the Trinitarian Light, O light-formed fathers, ye abandoned the deep darkness of passions; and as luminaries enlightening the hearts of the faithful by divine deeds, ye were shown forth blessed through fasting and sufferings: therefore today we honor your light-bearing and honorable memory, and with one accord cry aloud: O divinely speaking, most wealthy ones, pray Christ God to grant remission of sins unto those who in love honor your holy memory! *(Twice)*  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
As the all-immaculate Bride of the Creator, as the unwedded Mother of the Redeemer, as the receptacle of the Comforter, O all-hymned one: seeing that I have come to knowledge of myself as a foul dwelling of lawlessness and a playground of demons, make haste to deliver me from their evildoing, making me a bright dwelling through virtues: O light-bestowing and incorruptible one, disperse the cloud of passions, and vouchsafe me participation on high and the unwaning Light, by thy prayers!  
*Songs of Ascents, 1st Antiphon of Tone 4. Prokeimenon, Tone 4:*[^411]  
Precious in the sight of the Lord is the death of His venerable ones (Ps. 115:6).  
*Verse: What shall I render unto the Lord for all that He hath rendered unto me? (Ps. 115:3).*  
Let every breath praise the Lord.  
*Gospel from Matthew, Pericope 38 (Matt. 10:32–33, 37–38; 19:27–30).*[^412]  
*After Psalm 50, Sticheron, Tone 4:*[^413]  
With most radiant beams, O venerable ones, having enlightened your souls light-bearingly, with virtues ye adorned the earth, having Christ in your hearts: enlightening the assemblies of the gentiles as an unsetting sun, embellishing us with martyric beauties, O venerable martyrs!

*If thou celebratest with Polyeleos.*  
*Canon of the Theotokos on 4, and of the Venerable-Martyrs on 8.*[^414]  
*Tone 4. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^415]  
Traversing the red deep of the sea with unwetted feet, Israel of old, by the cross-shaped hands of Moses, conquered the power of Amalek in the wilderness.  
*Troparion 1:*  
Having become light-beaming through fasting, O most blessed ones, ye were shown forth as God-beholding luminaries by the shedding of your blood: therefore ye rejoice with the ascetics and martyrs of Christ.  
*Troparion 2:*  
In the sea of your tears, O wise ones, first having drowned the noetic Pharaoh, afterward in streams of blood ye conquered him and delivered him unto destruction.  
*Troparion 3:*  
O venerable fathers, ye were sacrificed for Christ, having lived ascetically; we praise the martyrs, singing unto our Redeemer God: For He is glorified!  
*Theotokion:*  
Being Lord by nature, uncontainable by all, He was contained in thee, O Virgin Mother of God, having become earthly for mercy's sake, that He might save earthly ones by His ineffable mercy.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*[^416]  
Not in wisdom and power and wealth do we boast, but in Thee, the hypostatic Wisdom of the Father, O Christ: for there is none holy save Thee, O Lover of mankind!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Having well-pleased God with a contrite conscience, ye crushed the enemy's the enemy's proud boastings, O blessed ones, being shattered and put to death by swords.
*Troparion 2:*  
Beholding the everlastingness of future things, O wise ones, ye brought to nought the unstable and corruptible things that drag downward: therefore are ye blessed, O venerable martyrs.  
*Troparion 3:*  
Boasting in Thy dominion, O God-bearing ones, they valiantly conquered the regiments of the destroyer, and being slain for Thy sake, O Savior, having shown forth twofold struggles, by them were they crowned.  
*Theotokion:*  
O all-pure one, who didst give birth unto the most holy Word of God: cleanse our souls and bodies, who call thee all-immaculate blessed.

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4. Podoben: "Speedily anticipate" (Ско́рѡ предварѝ):*[^417]  
As sojourners and strangers ye appeared upon earth; valiantly running into the deserts, ye struggled manfully, O venerable ones, adorned in nakedness as with a garment of virtue, conforming yourselves unto the sufferings of Christ: therefore did ye endure the tormentor's slaughter.  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
Unto the Mother of God let us now run diligently, we sinners and humble ones, and let us fall down in repentance, crying aloud from the depths of our soul: O Lady, help us, having had compassion upon us; make haste, for we perish from the multitude of our transgressions! Turn not Thy servants empty away, for thee alone do we have as hope!  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
Beholding upon the Cross the Lamb born of her without seed, pierced with a spear, wounded by arrows of sorrow, the Virgin and Ewe-lamb cried aloud, bitterly lamenting: What is this new mystery? How dost Thou die, Who art the only Lord of life? Therefore arise, raising up the fallen forefather!

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*[^418]  
Beholding Thee lifted up upon the Cross, O Sun of righteousness, the Church stood in her station, worthily crying aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Not being subject unto carnal passions, O most blessed ones, ye subjected the enemy; and being slain by the sword, rejoicing together ye were translated unto immortal life.  
*Troparion 2:*  
Having slain yourselves before death by self-mastery through your own will, O venerable ones: ye were cruelly cut down with the sword against your will by the lawless, believing that ye live even after death.  
*Troparion 3:*  
Even if ye are slain by swords by the lawless, O fathers, and stand apart from this life: yet before your departure ye endured bitter tribulations, being dead unto the world.  
*Theotokion:*  
Renewing mankind, the pre-eternal God appeared as a new child from the Virgin, and having become man, let us sing unto Him: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*[^419]  
Thou, my Lord, art come as a light into the world, a holy light, turning from dark ignorance those who praise Thee in faith.  
*Troparion 1:*  
A fair paradise ye appeared, having in your midst the Lord, the tree of life, Who received your blood as a pure sacrifice.  
*Troparion 2:*  
Having mingled drops of blood with tears, O God-bearers, in their streams ye drowned the multiform serpent.  
*Troparion 3:*  
Ye were vouchsafed to behold the beauty of God: and in place of labors and sicknesses ye received eternal rejoicing, O blessed ones.  
*Theotokion:*  
Thou didst give birth unto God, beyond word and understanding: and remained after childbirth, as also before childbirth, a Virgin, O pure Bride of God.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*[^420]  
I will sacrifice unto Thee with a voice of praise, O Lord, the Church cries unto Thee, having been cleansed from demonic blood by the blood that flowed for mercy's sake from Thy side.

*Troparion 1:*[^421]  
Having cast off the bond of attachment by fasting, and by violent death being loosed from corruptible bonds, ye were called unto the indestructible beauty of the Master.  
*Troparion 2:*  
Having shown yourselves as mountains by an exalted life, ye subjected beneath your feet by the power of Christ him who threatened to destroy the mountains and all creation.  
*Troparion 3:*  
By self-mastery and labors ye attenuated the body, O holy fathers; and with blood ye sacrificed yourselves valiantly unto Christ, and were lawfully crowned.  
*Theotokion:*  
Lull to sleep the unstable tempest of my passions by thy sleepless prayer, O Virgin, I pray thee: and grant me not to fall asleep with the heavy sleep of sin.

*Kontakion in the Typikon.*  
*If there be no Typikon, say this Kontakion, Tone 2. Podoben: "Seeking the highest" (Вы́шнихъ и҆щѧ̀):*[^422]  
Traversing the multitude of waves unharmed, with streams of your tears ye powerfully drowned the incorporeal foes, O divinely wise venerable-martyrs, and having received the gift of miracles: pray unceasingly for us all!  
*Ikos:*  
Those who on earth put the flesh to death with life-bearing mortification, having formed the holiness of Christ God, first in good fasting, and secondly again in sufferings: therefore Christ sent down twofold crowns upon you, O venerable martyrs, and prepared for you a heavenly dwelling and an eternal place, where ye joyfully rejoice, kindred of martyrs and venerable ones: therefore pray unceasingly for us all!

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*[^423]  
In the Persian furnace the Abrahamic youths, inflamed more by love of piety than by the flame, cried aloud: Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy glory, O Lord!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Adorned with virtuous beams and enlightened by sacred sufferings, ye passed over unto the unsetting Light, and were shown forth as sons of the day.  
*Troparion 2:*  
Separating yourselves from worldly pleasures by desire, ye were joined unto Him Who strangely appeared in the flesh unto men, having acquired future and incorruptible good things, O holy ones.  
*Troparion 3:*  
Blessed art Thou, O God, Who hast completed the course of the sufferers, the God-bearing fathers, today, and shown them forth as partakers of Thy heavenly kingdom.  
*Theotokion:*  
The jar that received manna of old truly prefigured thee manifestly, carrying in thy womb the living manna: blessed art thou among women, O all-immaculate Lady!

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*[^424]  
Daniel, having stretched forth his hands, shut the lions' gaping mouths in the pit; and the youths, lovers of piety, girt with virtue, quenched the power of fire, crying aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!  
*Troparion 1:*  
The great choir of venerable martyrs, adorned today with ascetic valor, shows forth all as divine dancers praising, and singing with them unto Christ: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Ye stood as lambs, being cut down by tormentors' swords, and were brought as a perfect sacrifice unto the Word Who was sacrificed, and appeared in the dwellings of the firstborn, singing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Ye sanctified body and soul unto the Lord, O venerable ones, being stirred by the sweat of self-mastery, and with streams of blood drying up the evil sea of the tormentor, singing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*Theotokion:*  
Through thee is the first paradise opened again, O pure one joyful in God, and man who was first condemned is led in, and truly deified unto renewal of men, who cry aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*[^425]  
A cornerstone was cut out without hands from an uncut mountain, even from thee, O Virgin, Christ, Who joined together the severed natures: therefore, rejoicing, we magnify thee, O Mother of God!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Come, let us praise with sacred chanting, with a loud voice, the sacred venerable fathers who lived in holiness in deserts and in caves, and suffered piously.  
*Troparion 2:*  
How most praiseworthy are your labors in asceticism, O passion-bearers! How excellent the struggles whereby ye cast down the deceiver! How marvelous the sufferings whereby ye lawfully suffered and were crowned!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Your God-beholding memory, illuminated by the dawn of your labors which ye gave for Christ, enlightens the counsels of all, by the grace of the Holy Spirit Who sees all.  
*Theotokion:*  
The Cherubim are terrified, beholding Him Who sits upon them by an ineffable word, the Word, carried as an infant by thy maternal hands, O all-pure Lady, higher than all creation.

*Exapostilarion:*  
*Podoben: "By the Spirit in the sanctuary" (Дх҃омъ во ст҃и́лищи):*[^426]  
By the pains of self-mastery ye utterly slew the author of evil, the serpent; and at your end ye received the crown of martyrdom, O praise of the fathers and glory of martyrs, multitude of venerable-martyrs, multitude of marvelous fathers!  
*Theotokion:*  
The most glorious wonder of thy childbirth, which is beyond understanding, astonishes every mind, both of angels and of men: being a Virgin thou gavest birth, a Virgin thou didst remain, as also before childbirth, O Virgin: O strange mystery! O marvelous and most glorious childbirth!

*If thou celebratest with Polyeleos: At the Praises, Stichera, Tone 4.*  
*Tone 4, Podoben: "As one valiant among martyrs" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́бла):*[^427]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
Let us brightly sing praise unto the wise martyrs and venerable fathers: for submitting unto the commands of Christ, and having brought to nought all foul service, the holy ones honorably and well-hopefully served the one God and Lord, courageously confessing before the face of tormentors, and being crowned from on high. *(Twice)*  
*Sticheron 2:*  
Having forsaken the things of life that drag downward, and the food of pleasure, and blooming glory, as temporal, O most praiseworthy ones: ye cleaved unto Christ, inflamed by His fair beauty, and approached as fragrant roses, and are divinely crowned with the crown of the incorruptible kingdom.  
*Sticheron 3:*  
Having hated the world, ye appeared otherworldly, gathered in the church of the firstborn: and with the angels ye sing an unceasing hymn, standing together before God; who martyrlike brought to nought the foul deception of idols, ye put to shame the madness of the tormentors.  
*Glory... Tone 6:*  
Adorned with the garment of purity, and enlightened by divine prayer: having Christ living in yourselves, Who was born of the Virgin, ye were not captivated by love of this world, nor did ye receive carnal pleasures: but ye ascended eagerly unto the tormenting fire, with divine fire, the temporal and passible and burned up the passible fire: therefore pray, O blessed venerable martyrs, that we also may be delivered from all-destructive and eternal fire!
*Both now... Theotokion:*  
We have known God Who was incarnate of thee, O Virgin Mother of God: pray Him for the salvation of our souls!  
*At the Liturgy, the Beatitudes from the Canon, Ode 3 and 6.*  
*Prokeimenon, Tone 7:*[^428]  
The venerable ones shall boast in glory, and they shall rejoice upon their beds (Ps. 149:5).  
*Verse: Sing unto the Lord a new song, His praise in the church of the venerable ones (Ps. 149:1).*  
*Epistle to the Romans, Pericope 94 (Rom. 8:14–21).*[^429]  
*Alleluia, Tone 6:*  
Blessed is the man that fears the Lord, in His commandments he will greatly desire (Ps. 111:1).  
*Verse: Mighty upon the earth shall be his seed (Ps. 111:2).*  
*Gospel from Matthew, Pericope 38 (Matt. 10:32–33, 37–38; 19:27–30); or Luke, Pericope 64 (Luke 12:8–12).*[^430]  
*Communion Hymn: Rejoice in the Lord, O ye righteous (Ps. 32:1).*[^431]

## Common Service to a Single Female Martyr

*At Vespers, at Lord, I have cried, Stichera, Tone 4.*  
*Tone 4, Podoben: "As one valiant among martyrs" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́бла):*[^432]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
With the beauties of virginity and with the blood of martyrdom having adorned thy soul, O glorious martyr [Name], thou wast betrothed unto thy Creator, Who truly preserves thee incorrupt unto ages: dancing therefore with the armies of archangels and angels, apostles, prophets, and martyrs, in choral assemblies, O all-praiseworthy one!  
*Sticheron 2:*  
Bound unto wheels, and torn asunder by wild beasts, and having sharpened thy mind with the divine Spirit through fire and water, with streams of blood thou didst manfully strangle the ruler of darkness; and thou didst ascend unto the noetic bridal chambers, bringing suffering as a dowry unto thy Bridegroom, O virgin!  
*Sticheron 3:*  
Having been cleansed by the blood of Christ the Bridegroom, O all-praiseworthy one, thou didst adorn the garment of the flesh with thy blood: therefore, enlightened on both sides, O all-praiseworthy [Name], thou wast vouchsafed His bright bridal chamber; pray Him to deliver from corruption and tribulations those who in faith perform thy most honorable memory!  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
Having unsleeping prayer and abiding intercession unto the Lord, O all-pure one, lull misfortunes to sleep, calm the waves of my wretched soul, and comfort my heart which is in affliction, O Maiden, I pray thee, and bestow grace upon my mind, that I may worthily glorify thee!  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
When the Ewe-lamb that gave Him birth beheld Thee, the Lamb and Shepherd, upon the Tree, she lamented and maternally spake unto Thee: O my desired Son, how art Thou suspended upon the Wood of the Cross, O longsuffering One? How are Thy hands and Thy feet nailed by the lawless, O Word, and Thou hast poured forth Thy blood, O Master?  
*If thou celebratest with Polyeleos: At Lord, I have cried, Stichera on 6.*  
*Glory... Tone 6:*[^433]  
At the right hand of the Savior stood the virgin, passion-bearer, and martyr [Name], clothed with invincible virtues, and adorned with the oil of purity and the blood of suffering, and crying unto Him, joyfully holding a lamp: I have run after the fragrance of Thy myrrh, O Christ God, for I am wounded by Thy love; separate me not, O heavenly Bridegroom By her prayers send down Thy mercies upon us, O all-powerful Savior!
*Both now... Theotokion:*  
Who doth not call thee blessed, O most holy Virgin?  
*Entrance. Prokeimenon of the day, and three readings (see fol. 45v):*[^434]  
*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 4. Podoben: "As one valiant among martyrs" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́бла):*[^435]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
Having manfully opposed the insolence and violent cruelty of the tormentor with thy mind, O divinely wise one, foreseeing the enjoyment of future things, which abides unshakeable forever: which also thou hast obtained, having passed over from earth unto the heavenly bridal chambers and the indestructible choir, O glorious one!  
*Verse: Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel (Ps. 67:36).*  
*Sticheron 2:*  
Thou wast vouchsafed to behold the majesty of the kingdom and the fair beauty of thy Bridegroom Christ: adorned with the wounds of thy firm suffering, thou didst worthily draw near unto the Fountain of good things; whence, O blessed one, thou hast richly received the fruit of divine rejoicing and immortal glory!  
*Verse: In the churches bless ye God, the Lord from the fountains of Israel (Ps. 67:27).*  
*Sticheron 3:*  
Neither the servile yoke, nor female weakness, nor famine, nor wounds deterred thee from being likened unto martyric firmness, O all-glorious one; with zeal of soul thou didst endure torments: whence thou hast obtained the heavenly bridal chamber, and art adorned with the crown of grace, standing before thy Creator!  
*Glory... Tone 5:*  
The King of glory, Christ, desired the beauty of thy virginity, and betrothed thee unto Himself as an immaculate bride in incorruptible union; for by His will He gave power unto thy beauty, and showed thee invincible against enemies and passions: having endured bitter wounds and fierce torments, He bound thee with a twofold crown, and set thee at His right hand as an adorned queen: pray Him, O honorable and much-suffering martyr [Name], to grant salvation, and life, and great mercy unto those who sing thy praise!  
*Both now... Theotokion:*  
Thou art the temple and the gate...  
*If thou celebratest not, say this Theotokion:*  
We call thee blessed, O Virgin Mother of God, and we the faithful glorify thee as is meet: the unshakeable city, the impregnable wall, the firm protectress and refuge of our souls!  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
Beholding of old her Lamb lifted up upon the Cross, the Virgin and all-blessed Maiden cried aloud, shedding tears: Woe is me, O my Son! How dost Thou die, Who art God, immortal by nature?  
*Troparion in the Typikon.*  
*If there be no Typikon, say this Troparion, Tone 4:*[^436]  
Thy lamb [Name], O Jesus, cries with a loud voice: Thee, my Bridegroom, do I love, and seeking Thee do I suffer, and am crucified, and am buried with Thy baptism; and I suffer for Thy sake, that I may reign in Thee, and I die for Thee, that I may also live with Thee: but receive me as a spotless sacrifice, sacrificed with love for Thee! By her prayers, as Thou art merciful, save our souls!  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion in the tone of the Troparion.*  
*At Matins, at God is the Lord: the same Troparion.*  
*After the 1st Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 5. Podoben: "Co-beginningless Word" (Собезна́чальное сло́во):*[^437]  
Having piously enslaved thyself unto the Almighty, thou didst not enslave thyself unto the impious tormentor, O honorable one, but didst firmly endure dark confinement and the burning of wounds, and wast translated unto God, O divinely wise martyr [Name]: pray for us who celebrate thy memory! *(Twice)*
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
O fervent protectress and invincible, certain and unashamed hope, wall and protection and harbor of those who run unto thee, O pure Ever-Virgin: pray thy Son and God with the angels to grant peace unto the world, and salvation, and great mercy!  
*After the 2nd Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 1. Podoben: "Thy tomb, O Savior" (Гро́бъ тво́й Сп҃се):*[^438]  
With the dew of the Spirit thou didst quench the fire of torments, O honorable one, and wast translated unto the divine and immaterial Light, and after thy blessed end thou pourest forth drops of healings, [Name], staying the burning of passions by spiritual power! *(Twice)*  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
Accept the prayers of thy servants, O Mother of God, and deliver us from every tribulation, as thou gavest birth unto Christ the Savior, the Redeemer of our souls!  
*At the Polyeleos, Megalynarion:*[^439]  
We magnify thee, O passion-bearer of Christ [Name], and we honor thy honorable suffering, which thou didst endure for Christ!  
*After the Polyeleos, Sessional Hymn, Tone 4. Podoben: "Lifted up upon the Cross" (Вознесы́йся на крⷭ҇тъ во́лею):*[^440]  
Come, ye multitude of the faithful, let us divinely praise [Name], who came willingly unto Christ and adorned her heart with virtues: for she put to shame the insolence of the tormentor, and having passed as a sun through the midst of the lawless, after her end was shown forth as a divine confirmation and strength unto earthly ones! *(Twice)*  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
Receiving the word from the angel in thy womb, and having given birth unto the incarnate God Emmanuel: O Mother of God, pray for our souls!  
*Songs of Ascents, 1st Antiphon of Tone 4. Prokeimenon:*  
Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel (Ps. 67:36).  
*Verse: In the churches bless ye God, the Lord from the fountains of Israel (Ps. 67:27).*  
Let every breath praise the Lord.  
*Gospel from Mark, Pericope 21 (Mark 5:24–34).*[^441]  
*After Psalm 50, Sticheron, Tone 6:*  
At the right hand of the Savior stood the virgin... *(Written at Lord, I have cried, at Glory).*  
*If thou celebratest with Polyeleos:*  
*Canon of the Theotokos on 4, and of the Female Martyr on 8.*  
*Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^442]  
Let us sing unto the Lord, Who led His people through the Red Sea: for He alone is gloriously glorified!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Unto hymning the all-praiseworthy maiden draws the hosts of otherworldly ones and the choirs of earthly ones by her wonders.  
*Troparion 2:*  
The Master of all, having desired the beauty of thy most fair heart, O all-praiseworthy one, vouchsafed thee the heavenly bridal chambers.  
*Troparion 3:*  
Unto punishments and diseases and manifold wounds thou didst draw near undaunted, O martyr: for thou hadst the Savior's grace helping thee and strengthening thee.  
*Theotokion:*  
O all-pure Mother of God, who gave birth beyond nature unto the incarnate, co-eternal, and pre-existing Word: we sing praise unto thee!  
*Ode 3. Heirmos:*[^443]  
Thou art the confirmation of those who run unto Thee, O Lord; Thou art the light of the darkened, and my spirit sings praise unto Thee!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Thou didst appear at the tribunal, bearing a courageous soul, and the enemy and the enemy as one unmanly thou didst conquer, O all-praiseworthy one!
*Troparion 2:*  
There was no blemish in thy beauty, nor wrinkle in thy soul; and Christ received thee as a bride into the incorruptible bridal chambers.  
*Troparion 3:*  
Heal the wounds of my soul, O all-praiseworthy martyr of Christ [Name], and by thy prayers calm the tempest of my life!  
*Theotokion:*  
We Christians have all acquired thee as our refuge and wall, and unceasingly glorify thee, O unwedded one!

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 8. Podoben: "Of wisdom" (Премꙋ́дрости):*[^444]  
With streams of thy blood, O martyr of Christ, thou ever causest a deluge for the impious, O all-praiseworthy one; and watering with showers of grace the noetic fields at all times, in them thou causest the grain of faith to grow: therefore most gloriously even after death hast thou appeared as a cloud pouring forth the testimony of life, O all-praiseworthy passion-bearer [Name]: pray Christ God to grant remission of sins unto those who in love honor thy holy memory!  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
As a Virgin and the only one among women, who without seed gavest birth unto God in the flesh, all we generations of men call thee blessed; for the fire of the Godhead made its abode in thee, and thou sucklest as an infant the Creator and Lord: therefore we, the angelic and human race, worthily glorify thy most holy childbearing, and with one accord cry unto thee: Pray Christ God to grant remission of sins unto those who worthily sing thy glory!  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
Beholding her Lamb and Shepherd and Redeemer upon the Cross, the Ewe-lamb cried aloud, weeping, and bitterly lamenting, proclaimed: The world indeed rejoices, receiving redemption through Thee, but my womb burns, beholding Thy crucifixion, which Thou endurest for mercy's sake, O longsuffering Lord, abyss of mercy and inexhaustible fountain: have compassion, and grant remission of transgressions unto those who in faith praise Thy divine sufferings!

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*[^445]  
I have heard, O Lord, the mystery of Thy dispensation, I have understood Thy works, and I have glorified Thy Godhead!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Having made thyself an undefiled mirror of divine understandings, as a most splendid golden luminary thou didst shine forth in the midst of sufferers, O martyr!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Thou didst not offer sacrifices unto the dark demon, O invincible martyr [Name]: for thou didst love to receive a life-bearing death for the sake of piety.  
*Troparion 3:*  
As one painlessly bearing the body with the passion-bearers, O immaculate one, thou didst remain insensible to wounds, through longing for divine love.  
*Theotokion:*  
Grant us cleansing from ignorance, as Thou art sinless, and give peace unto Thy world, O God, through the prayers of her that gave Thee birth!

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*[^446]  
Rising early, we cry unto Thee, O Lord, save us: for Thou art our God, beside Thee we know no other!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Knowing the opposite of the day and of the world, thou didst not wish to love the war-loving demon.  
*Troparion 2:*  
Thinking to weaken thy divine strength, O martyr [Name], the all-crafty one was shown forth as a laughing-stock by his wiles.  
*Troparion 3:*  
Grant me enlightenment and peace, O all-praiseworthy one, loosing by thy prayers my turbulent confusion!  
*Theotokion:*  
We praise thee as a Virgin after childbirth, O Mother of God: for thou didst bear God the Word in the flesh unto the world.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*[^447]  
Grant me a radiant robe, Thou that deckest Thyself with light as with a garment, O most merciful Christ our God!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Bearing a courageous mindset of soul in female flesh, O glorious one, thou didst not care for the beasts that were in the waters.  
*Troparion 2:*  
Thou didst conquer the pride of the tormentors, and didst remain unharmed, O invincible martyr, and didst receive the crown of victory.  
*Troparion 3:*  
As good, and as fair, and as honorable, and as shining with the beams of virginity, the Bridegroom and Lord leads thee unto Himself, O most glorious one!  
*Theotokion:*  
Thou who alone didst give birth unto the Word in the flesh through the Word: deliver our souls from the snares of the enemy, we pray!

*Kontakion in the Typikon.*  
*If there be no Typikon, say this Kontakion, Tone 2. Podoben: "Seeking the highest" (Вы́шнихъ и҆щѧ̀):*[^448]  
Finding thy most honorable church as a spiritual healing, all we faithful cry aloud unto thee with a great voice, O virgin martyr [Name], greatly renowned: Pray Christ God unceasingly for all of us!  
*Ikos:*  
Betrothed unto Christ by martyrdom, [Name], coming together let us worthily honor thee: that by her prayers being delivered from soul-corrupting destruction, pestilence, earthquake, and ruin, we may pass our life in humility, having been vouchsafed by the holy times of those who from ages past have well-pleased God, to walk in the light and to sing with them: Marvelous hast Thou shown Thy mercies unto all men, O Savior, who praise Thee in faith: therefore do we cry unto her: Pray unceasingly for all of us!

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*[^449]  
The youths that went down from Judea to Babylon of old, by faith in the Trinity trampled down the flame of the furnace, singing: O God of the fathers, blessed art Thou!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Beyond understanding was shown forth the noble courage of the divine martyrs: for the Creator of all punished creation, while they in their suffering cried: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou!  
*Troparion 2:*  
The most glorious maiden stopped the babbling mouths of the tormentors and the pride of the most lawless, divinely singing by the Holy Spirit: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou!  
*Troparion 3:*  
The trinity of venerable youths burned up those that kindled the furnace of old; and now the divinely wise maiden, singing unto the Trinity, captured the servants, singing unto the God of the fathers: O God, blessed art Thou!  
*Theotokion:*  
When Thou wast pleased to accomplish our salvation, O Savior, Thou didst make Thy dwelling in the womb of the Virgin, whom Thou didst show forth as the protectress of the world: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou!

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*[^450]  
The King of heaven, Whom all the angelic hosts praise: praise and exalt Him above all forever!  
*Troparion 1:*  
The most blessed among women, having reward-giving grace from on high, sang, praising Christ forever!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Strengthened by the thought of the Bridegroom, through immaterial longing thou didst surrender the body unto death, and livest forever!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Mystically the Bridegroom came unto the all-pure bride in the furnace, and by spiritual dew through the Father's good pleasure saved her, as she sang unto Christ forever.  
*Theotokion:*  
Despise not those who need help from thee, O Virgin, who sing and exalt thee above all forever!

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*[^451]  
Thee, the unwedded Mother of God on high; thee, who beyond understanding gavest birth truly unto God by the Word, higher than the all-pure hosts: with unceasing glorifications do we magnify!  
*Troparion 1:*  
The stream of living blood, a sign of the incorruptible life granted unto thee from on high, O all-praiseworthy martyr: for thou wast shown forth as an unceasing treasure of healings unto those who draw from it.  
*Troparion 2:*  
Death touched thee according to the law of nature, O divinely wise one: but impassibly thou didst clothe thyself in life-bearing mortification, whereby, having laid down thy all-honorable body as living, thou livest incorruptibly, and thy blood-streamings bear witness!  
*Troparion 3:*  
As a sunbeam illuminating all, and as the most excellent bride of Christ, as a pure turtle-dove, and as an olive-tree, as a cedar, as a sacred chosen dove, all we praise thee, [Name], divinely named!  
*Theotokion:*  
O unwedded Bride, receptacle of fragrance, thee as true and immaculate, and cloud of divine Light, who didst receive heavenly rain in thy womb, O Virgin Mother, we magnify!

*Exapostilarion:*  
As a bride thou wast adorned with beauty, having betrothed thyself unto the Bridegroom by virtue-working sufferings: and within the light-formed bridal chamber as a chosen one thou didst make thine abode, carrying a virgins' lamp; and now thou reignest shining with Him Who lives unto ages, [Name]!  
*Theotokion:*  
All we sinners possess thee as our protection, O all-pure Virgin: make thy Son propitious unto us by thy maternal prayers!

*If thou celebratest with Polyeleos:*  
*At the Praises, Stichera Automela, Tone 3:*[^452]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
Beholding the martyric festival divinely wise being accomplished, O ye faithful, let us sing a thanksgiving hymn unto our God Who is marvelous in counsels: for He conquered the invisible dominion of the opposing power in female nature, and by His divine power brought to perfection the good martyrs in weakness: by her prayers He saves our souls! *(Twice)*  
*Sticheron 2:*  
Having mingled the cup of truth from her martyric blood, the all-praiseworthy martyr of Christ [Name], ever setting it forth unto the Church, therein convenes the nurslings of the Church with the word of wisdom, saying: Draw the drink that witnesses the Resurrection, that drives away the unbelieving, that purifies passions, and preserves pious souls, crying unto the Savior: O Thou Who hast given us to drink of the torrent of the sweetness of the Spirit, save our souls!  
*Sticheron 3:*  
Having sealed our souls with the blood of Christ in the day of redemption, let us prophetically draw with spiritual rejoicing the holy blood flowing unto us from the martyric fountain, prefiguring the life-flowing sufferings of the Savior and eternal glory: therefore let us cry aloud unto Him: O Lord Who art glorified in Thy saints, by the prayers of Thy most glorious passion-bearer save our souls!  
*Glory... Tone 6:*[^453]  
Having walked the path of suffering, thou didst escape the counsel of the tormentor, O most honorable [Name]: as a wise virgin, carrying a lamp, thou didst enter into the courts of thy Lord; as a martyr by courage thou didst receive grace to heal carnal passions: but also deliver from spiritual sicknesses us who sing us who sing thy praise, by thy prayers unto God!
*Both now... Theotokion:*  
We have known God Who was incarnate of thee, O Virgin Mother of God: pray Him for the salvation of our souls!  
*At the Liturgy, the Beatitudes from the Canon, Ode 3 and 6.*  
*Prokeimenon, Tone 4:*  
Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel (Ps. 67:36).  
*Verse: In the churches bless ye God, the Lord from the fountains of Israel (Ps. 67:27).*  
*Epistle to the Corinthians, Pericope 181 (1 Cor. 7:24–35); or to the Galatians, Pericope 208 (Gal. 3:23–4:5).*[^454]  
*Alleluia, Tone 4:*  
With patience I waited for the Lord, and He gave ear unto me, and heard my prayer (Ps. 39:2).  
*Gospel from Matthew, Pericope 92 (Matt. 22:35–46); or Mark, Pericope 21 (Mark 5:24–34).*[^455]  
*Communion Hymn: In everlasting memory shall the righteous be (Ps. 111:6b).*[^456]

## Common Service to Two and Many Female Martyrs

*At Vespers, at Lord, I have cried, Stichera, Tone 4.*  
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Thou hast given a sign" (Да́лъ є҆сѝ зна́менїе):*[^457]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
The virgin maidens, bound by the law of nature and certainly strengthened by love for their Creator, loosed the bond of bodily things by faith; and the powerless enemy they manfully crushed with their feet, and were radiantly adorned with victorious honor, and joyfully made their abode in the noetic bridal chamber.  
*Sticheron 2:*  
Enduring fire and manifold torments and death, O most honorable ones, possessing by faith the Bridegroom's most fair beauty, and adorned with diverse wounds, they were joined unto Him: therefore Jesus, the Lover of mankind and Savior of our souls, crowned them with many gifts!  
*Sticheron 3:*  
Incorruptible virginity, most marvelous virginity ye brought unto Christ, O good virgins, having cast down the insolence of the deception of godlessness by the power of the Cross with a manly mind: therefore all the churches of Christ celebrate your holy memory, radiant and most glorious!  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
