Having unsleeping prayer and abiding intercession unto the Lord, O all-pure one, lull misfortunes to sleep, calm the waves of my wretched soul, and comfort my heart which is in affliction, O Maiden, I pray thee, and bestow grace upon my mind, that I may worthily glorify thee!  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
When the Ewe-lamb that gave Him birth beheld Thee, the Lamb and Shepherd, upon the Tree, she lamented and maternally spake unto Thee: O my most desired Son, how art Thou suspended upon the Wood of the Cross, O longsuffering One? How are Thy hands and Thy feet nailed by the lawless, O Word, and Thou hast poured forth Thy blood, O Master?  
*If thou celebratest with Polyeleos: At Lord, I have cried, Stichera on 6.*  
*Glory... Tone 8:*[^458]  
Let every tongue be moved to the praise of the all-praiseworthy female martyrs, every generation and age; let us, all young men and virgins, crown the great-martyrs of Christ with praises! For having lawfully shown courage and cast off female weakness, they cast down the enemy tormentor: adorned on account of their martyric labors with heavenly divine crowns, they ask of their Bridegroom and God to grant us great mercy!

*Both now... Theotokion:*  
The King of heaven, for His love toward mankind...[^459]  
*Entrance. Prokeimenon of the day. And three readings.*  
*Look in the service of martyrs, leaf 84 on the reverse (see fol. 84v).*[^460]  
*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 4.*  
*Tone 4, Podoben: "As one valiant" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́блѧ):*[^461]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
At your heartfelt yearning, divine love, and union in suffering, O blessed ones, the angelic choirs were truly astonished: how in manly combat, having cast down the invisible enemy, ye worthily and manifestly received the crowns of victory from the life-bearing hand!  
*Verse: Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel (Ps. 67:36).*  
*Sticheron 2:*  
Being brought to completion in Christ, by your burning wisdom ye quenched the vain flame, and having kept the lamp of your souls unquenchable, ye entered with Christ into the heavenly bridal chamber: therefore all we piously hymn your holy memory, O passion-bearers, being sanctified by grace!  
*Verse: In the churches bless ye God, the Lord from the fountains of Israel (Ps. 67:27).*  
*Sticheron 3:*  
Having endured the sufferings of many torments, O all-praiseworthy ones, having run with strength, from hence ye were translated unto the painless reception of unwaning radiance, bright joy, and eternal divine delight: therefore we bless you, and today celebrate your holy solemnity, O divinely honored passion-bearers!  
*Glory... Tone 4:*  
Having forsaken the enjoyment of food and the blossoming glory of the life that drags downward as temporary, O all-praiseworthy ones, ye cleaved unto Christ through martyrdom, being inflamed by His fair beauty; and ye drew near like fragrant sweet-briars, and were crowned with the crown of the incorruptible Kingdom, O divinely named ones!  
*Both now... Theotokion:*  
Look upon the petitions of thy servants...[^462]  
*And if thou celebratest not, speak this Theotokion:*  
O unquenchable lamp, righteous throne, all-pure Lady, pray that our souls may be saved!  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
Beholding Christ, the Lover of mankind, being crucified and pierced in the ribs with a spear, the all-pure one wept, crying aloud: What is this, O my Son? What have the ungrateful people rendered unto Thee in return for the good things that Thou didst for them? And Thou hastenest to make me childless, O most beloved One! I marvel, O compassionate One, at Thy voluntary crucifixion!  
*Troparion in the Typikon.*  
*And if there be no Typikon, speak this Troparion, Tone 4:*  
As spiritual ewe-lambs ye were brought by martyrdom unto Christ the Lamb and Shepherd, having finished the course and kept the faith: therefore today with a joyful soul we celebrate your holy memory, O wonderfully esteemed ones, magnifying Christ!  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion, according to the tone of the Troparion.*  
*At Matins, at God is the Lord, the same Troparion.*  
*After the 1st Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 4.*  
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Thy tomb, O Savior" (Гро́бъ тво́й Сп҃се):*[^463]  
Kindled with the fire of divine longing, by the touch of physical fire ye were not scorched at all, O blessed ones; but ye burned up the deception, and being dragged unmercifully, O ever-memorable passion-bearers, ye received your end, and obtained glory! *(Twice)*  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
Guide us into the way of repentance, who ever turn aside unto evil pathlessness, and anger the most good Lord, O unwedded, blessed Mary, refuge of despondent men, O dwelling-place of God!
*After the 2nd Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 4.*  
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Joseph marveled" (ᲊди́висѧ І҆ѡ́сифъ):*[^464]  
The heavenly choirs of the bodiless powers marveled at your great endurance: for as though another were suffering, ye remained while your shins and joints were broken, enduring a bitter death, and in a female body crushing the apostate serpent, O virgins, brides of the Giver of life, obedient ones of the faith! *(Twice)*  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
Joseph marveled, beholding that which was beyond nature, and he considered in his mind the rain upon the fleece in thy seedless conception, O Mother of God: the bush unburnt by fire, Aaron's rod that budded; and thy betrothed and guardian bore witness, crying unto the priests: A Virgin gives birth, and after giving birth remains again a Virgin!  
*At the Polyeleos, Megalynarion:*  
We magnify you, O holy passion-bearers, and we honor your precious sufferings, which ye endured for Christ!  
*Selected Psalm: God is our refuge and strength (Ps. 45:2).*  
*After the Polyeleos, Sessional Hymn, Tone 8.*  
*Tone 8, Podoben: "The Wisdom and Word" (Премꙋ́дрости):*[^465]  
Having betrothed yourselves unto the Lord in a manner fitting God, as a gift ye brought Him blood and slaughter, O passion-bearing maidens, and worthily attained the divine bridal chamber, being unceasingly filled with ineffable illumination: therefore, spiritually observing your holy and honorable memory, we glorify the Savior, and faithfully cry aloud: Pray Christ God to grant remission of sins unto those who with love honor your holy memory! *(Twice)*  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
By the prayers of Thine incorporeal hosts, O Christ, and of Thy Forerunner, of the Apostles, Prophets, and Martyrs, and of all the Saints, and of the Mother of God, being entreated by Thine unwedded and good Mother, grant us to walk in Thy light, and make us worthy to attain Thy Kingdom, according to the compassion of Thy mercy!  
*Gradual Hymns, the 1st Antiphon of Tone 4. Prokeimenon:*  
Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel (Ps. 67:36).  
*Verse: In the churches bless ye God, the Lord from the fountains of Israel (Ps. 67:27).*  
*Let every breath praise the Lord.*  
*Gospel from Matthew, Pericope 104 (Matt. 25:1–13).*[^466]  
*After Psalm 50, Sticheron, Tone 4:*  
Having forsaken the enjoyment of food and the blossoming glory of the life that drags downward...  
*It is written at the Aposticha, Glory.*  
*If thou celebratest with Polyeleos:*  
*Canon of the Mother of God on 4, and of the female martyrs on 8.*  
*Tone 4. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^467]  
I shall open my mouth, and it will be filled with the Spirit, and I shall pour forth a word unto the Queen Mother, and I shall appear brightly celebrating, and rejoicing I shall sing her wonders!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Ye fought stoutly against the adversary, O martyrs, first by fasting, and secondly by bloody sufferings: therefore with faith we honor your memory!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Being wounded with longing for Him Who endured scourges and death for our sake, the venerable female martyrs followed His steps, having forgotten bodily weakness!  
*Troparion 3:*  
With the weapon of suffering ye cast down the pagan sacrificial places and the demonic arrays, and ye were brought unto the heavenly Church as living whole-burnt offerings, O most honorable female martyrs!  
*Theotokion:*  
Being strengthened by the grace of Him Who shone forth from thy womb, O all-immaculate one from thy womb, O all-immaculate one, the virgin maidens dashed against the pains of martyrdom, and were brought after thee rejoicing!
*Ode 3. Heirmos:*[^468]  
The bow of the mighty has grown weak, and the infirm have girded themselves with strength: therefore my heart is established in the Lord!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Strengthened by strong power from God, they trampled down the strength of the opposing adversary: and therefore as invincible victors they were glorified!  
*Troparion 2:*  
The gaping jaws of the beasts were bound idle by the divine power of Christ: and ye were delivered unharmed, O divinely wise ones, glorifying God!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Having illuminated your mind with wisdom and grace, ye were not terrified by the threats of the tormentors, O passion-bearers of the Savior, being strengthened by the power of God!  
*Theotokion:*  
Having understood with pious faith, we proclaim thee as the true Mother of God, all-immaculate: for through thee the Creator was well pleased to unite Himself with us!  
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 1:*  
Having gained you as fountains of miracles, we draw health abundantly, O all-praiseworthy female martyrs, hymning your pains, and divine zeal, and valiant wounds and struggles: and we celebrate your wonderfully esteemed memory, unto the glory of our God!  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
Accept the prayers of thy servants, O Mother of God, and deliver us from every distress: for thou hast given birth unto Christ the Savior, the Deliverer of our souls!  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
Possessing thy protection, O all-pure one, and by thy prayers being delivered from grievous things, being preserved everywhere by the Cross of thy Son, as is due we all piously magnify thee!  
*Ode 4. Heirmos:*[^469]  
He Who sits in glory upon the throne of Godhead, Jesus the most divine, came upon a light cloud, and with incorruptible hand saved those who cry: Glory to Thy power, O Christ!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Having endured torments and bodily wounds in diverse manners, the crushing of limbs, and burns, ye inherited the heavenly mansions, having tasted of the tree of life, O wonderfully esteemed ones!  
*Troparion 2:*  
The heavenly powers marveled at the struggle of the blessed female martyrs: how they conquered the enemy in female nature, being strengthened by the power of Him Who shone forth from the Virgin!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Having cast away all worldly vanity, ye cleaved unto God alone with your whole soul: therefore ye endured ascetic labors and torments, O patient brides of Christ!  
*Theotokion:*  
The Lord descended into thy womb, O all-immaculate one, as rain upon the fleece, as the prophet said of old, Whom thou hast given birth unto in two essences; unto Whom we cry: Glory to Thy power, O Christ!  
*Ode 5. Heirmos:*[^470]  
The ungodly shall not behold Thy glory, O Christ; but we, waking early from the night, hymn Thee, the Lover of mankind, the Only-mighty One, the Effulgence of the Father's glory of the Godhead!  
*Troparion 1:*  
As spotless ewe-lambs and as acceptable sacrifices ye were brought unto Christ the true Shepherd, perfect offerings and acceptable whole-burnt sacrifices!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Being put to death in the body, ye were made alive in the soul, O female martyrs, likening yourselves unto Him Who put to death the might of death, and endured the Cross, death, and voluntary suffering!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Possessing one mind in many bodies, with diverse wounds scraped and burned with fire, the divinely wise ones confessed the one Lord of all, the Trinity!
*Theotokion:*  
Raise me up who have fallen into the pit of passions, and guide me, O only all-immaculate one who gavest birth unto God and the Ruler Who by grace united those who were formerly divided!  
*Ode 6. Heirmos:*[^471]  
I shall sacrifice unto Thee with the voice of praise, O Lord, the Church cries unto Thee, having been cleansed from the demonic blood by the blood which for mercy's sake flowed from Thy ribs!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Beholding the serpent broken and trampled under female feet, the foremother divinely rejoices, whom of old by subtlety he cast out of Paradise!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Having united fasting unto lawful suffering, ye have now incorruptibly cleaved unto the Bridegroom of souls, and with a joyful soul ye dwell in the heavenly bridal chamber!  
*Troparion 3:*  
The surging waves of torment were not able to sink the martyric ships: for by a strong hand they reached the divine haven!  
*Theotokion:*  
Beholding the fulfillment of thy words, O Mother of God, we magnify thee on high and Him Who magnified thee: for truly all generations now call thee blessed!  
*Kontakion in the Typikon.*  
*And if there be no Typikon, speak this Kontakion, Tone 4.*  
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Thou hast appeared today" (Ꙗ҆ви́лся є҆сѝ дне́сь):*[^472]  
We celebrate the memory of the passion-bearers of Christ, and asking help with faith that all may be delivered from every affliction, we cry aloud: Our God is with us, Who glorified them according as He was well pleased!  
*Ikos:*  
Having gained you as fountains of miracles, we draw health abundantly, O martyrs of Christ, honoring your pains, and divine zeal, and valiant wounds and struggles; and we celebrate your wonderfully esteemed memory, crying aloud: Our God is with us, Who glorified them according as He was well pleased!  
*Ode 7. Heirmos:*[^473]  
O Thou Who didst save Thy children of Abraham in the fire and didst slay the Chaldeans, whom justice righteously overtook: O all-hymned Lord God of our fathers, blessed art Thou!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Having the eyes of their heart set upon God and King Who saves, the ewe-lambs of Christ entered against all the subtle and malicious hosts, and conquered manfully, crying aloud: Blessed is the God of our fathers!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Having all your desire directed toward the one Master, O female martyrs, ye accounted the beauties of this life as a dream, crying aloud: Blessed is the God of our fathers!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Let us not faint, behold the bridal chamber is opened, but rather let us stand manfully: for Christ stretches forth crowns, and let us not spare our bodies! cried the valiant female martyrs in the judgment hall.  
*Theotokion:*  
Blessed is the Fruit of thy blessed womb, O pure one, Whom the heavenly hosts and assemblies of men bless, Who delivered us from the first curse, O blessed one!  
*Ode 8. Heirmos:*[^474]  
The birthgiving of the Mother of God saved the pious children in the furnace; then prefigured, but now fulfilled, it moves the whole inhabited world to sing unto Thee: Hymn the Lord, O ye works, and exalt Him above all unto all ages!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Ye clothed yourselves in radiant garments with the blood of martyrdom, having truly put off the man corrupted by sin, singing: Hymn the Lord, O ye works, and exalt Him above all unto ages!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Enlightened by the divine brightnesses divine brightnesses of the rich light of the noetic Sun, O all-praiseworthy female martyrs, ye passed through the night of godlessness, singing in unity of soul: Hymn the Lord, O ye works, and exalt Him above all unto ages!
*Troparion 3:*  
As ewe-lambs, and as undefiled heifers, as God's doves, as voluntary offerings, ye were brought unto the Creator, O female martyrs, as an immaculate sacrifice, harmoniously singing: Hymn the Lord, O ye works, and exalt Him above all unto ages!  
*Theotokion:*  
The women passion-bearers ran after thee, longing for the fragrant myrrh of Thine only-begotten Son Who shone forth from thy womb, O all-pure Bride of God, who alone knewest not wedlock; and with thee they truly reign, hymning Christ unto ages!  
*Ode 9. Heirmos:*[^475]  
Eve by the disease of disobedience brought in the curse; but thou, O Virgin Mother of God, by the blossoming of thy bearing in the womb, didst make blessing to bud forth unto the world: therefore we all magnify thee!  
*Troparion 1:*  
The most glorious female martyrs grant drops of healings unto those in need as from a fountain, they assuage painful passions, and drive away hosts of demons; and the hearts of the godly they water unto the fruitful bearing of divine deeds!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Ye were lifted up, having received strength against enemies, and were likened unto angels; ye unhinderedly enjoy the tree of life in Paradise, O brides of God, having received the Fountain of good things, praying for the world!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Your memory, O passion-bearers, shining with divine light-bearing graces, enlightens the mind of those who praise you!  
*Theotokion:*  
Dwelling-place of Wisdom, higher than mind thou wast shown to be more excellent, O immaculate Virgin, a living throne and door: therefore women loved thee as Queen, and were brought after thee!  
*Exapostilarion:*  
Longing by every means to behold the Bridegroom's beauty, by turning toward Him, O divinely inspired ones, ye were honored with immortality in mortal corruption: therefore ye are worthily called blessed!  
*Theotokion:*  
Spare me, O Christ, when Thou wilt judge the world with glory: dispel the gloom of my passions, by the prayers of her that bare Thee, and of Thy precious female martyrs, as Thou art good and of great mercy!  
*If thou celebratest with Polyeleos:*  
*At the Praises, Stichera, Tone 4.*  
*Tone 4, Podoben: "As one valiant" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́блѧ):*[^476]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
Adorning yourselves with bloody crimson dyings, O virgin maidens, ye were incorruptibly united unto Christ our God Who is fair in beauty, Who preserves your virginity undefiled in the immortal bridal chamber of incorruption, in the eternal mansions, in the chamber not made with hands, O all-praiseworthy female martyrs! *(Twice)*  
*Sticheron 2:*  
With bodies imperfect indeed in age, but with perfect mind, by the power of the Spirit ye conquered the ancient, evil-originating serpent, O glorious ones, and showed his strength to be powerless: therefore ye received the crowns of victory, O all-praiseworthy female martyrs, obedient servants of the Trinity!  
*Sticheron 3:*  
Both broken into limbs, and consumed by fire, and torn by iron claws, and suspended upon a tree, and cut down with the sword, ye denied not Christ, O all-praiseworthy passion-bearers: therefore ye received the crown of victory, O great and most rich female martyrs, obedient servants of the Trinity!

*Glory... Tone 2:*  
Having lived an undefiled life, having overthrown the godless tribunal, ye stood bearing victory, O honorable ones; bearing as a flower the brightness of God, and clothed in the power of God, ye derided the command of the tormentors, and spat upon the prating words of the orators, O divinely wise female martyrs!  
*Both now... Theotokion:*  
All my hope I place upon thee, O Mother of God: preserve me under thy shelter!  
*At the Liturgy, the Beatitudes from the Canon, Ode 3 and 6.*  
*Prokeimenon, Tone 4:*  
Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel (Ps. 67:36).  
*Verse: In the churches bless ye God, the Lord from the fountains of Israel (Ps. 67:27).*  
*Epistle to the Corinthians, Pericope 181 (1 Cor. 7:24–35); or to the Galatians, Pericope 208 (Gal. 3:23–4:5).*[^477]  
*Alleluia, Tone 4:*  
With patience I waited for the Lord, and He gave ear unto me, and heard my prayer (Ps. 39:2).  
*Gospel from Matthew, Pericope 92 (Matt. 22:35–46); or Mark, Pericope 21 (Mark 5:24–34).*[^478]  
*Communion Hymn: Rejoice in the Lord, O ye righteous (Ps. 32:1).*[^479]

## Common Service to a Single Venerable Holy Woman

*At Vespers, at Lord, I have cried, Stichera, Tone 8.*  
*Tone 8, Podoben: "Thy martyrs, O Lord" (Мꙋ́ченицы твоѝ Гдⷭ҇и):*[^480]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
Enduring in ascetic pains, thou didst receive grace to cleanse spiritual and bodily sicknesses, O honorable mother [Name], and to drive away unclean spirits by the Spirit, and to intercede for all who are in affliction: therefore by thy prayers ask healing for all, and great mercy!  
*Sticheron 2:*  
The shrine of thy relics pours forth recovery of sight unto the blind, and healing unto all who are sick, who come with faith and ask thy visitation, O venerable and wonderfully esteemed mother [Name]: therefore by thy prayers ask for us great mercy!  
*Sticheron 3:*  
Thou didst acquire compassion toward thy neighbor, orthodox faith, and love toward God, O divinely blessed and honorable [Name]: for this cause the spiritual grace of God rested in thee, O venerable mother; therefore by thy prayers preserve those who bless thee with faith!  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
We pray thee, our protectress, who are held fast in sorrows, despise not thy servants that they perish utterly; but make haste to deliver us from the present wrath and affliction, O all-pure, spotless one who containedst God: for thou art for us an invincible wall and help!  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
Beholding Thee nailed upon the Cross, O Jesus, and willingly accepting sufferings, the Virgin and Thy Mother, O Master, cried out: Woe is me, my sweet Child! How dost Thou endure wounds unrighteously, O Physician Who healedst the infirmity of man and deliveredst all from corruption by Thy compassion?  
*If thou celebratest with Polyeleos: At Lord, I have cried, Stichera on 6.*  
*Glory... Tone 2:*  
The snaring of the soul and the carnal passions thou didst cut down with the sword of abstinence, and mental sins thou didst choke by the silence of fasting; and with the streams of thy tears thou didst water the whole wilderness, and didst cause the fruits of repentance to bud forth for us: therefore we celebrate thy memory, O venerable one!  
*Both now... Theotokion:*  
The shadow of the law has passed away, now that grace has come...[^481]  
*Entrance. Prokeimenon of the day. And three readings.*  
*Look in the Service to Venerable Monastics, leaf 2 on the reverse (see fol. 2v).*[^482]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 1.*  
*Tone 1, Podoben: "Joy of the heavenly hosts" (Небе́сныхъ чинѡ́въ):*[^483]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
Truly desiring the glory of the Fathers, thou didst love the incorruptible glory: therefore, forsaking sweet things, thou gavest over thy body unto all pains, O [Name], and now thou receivest the recompense of pains, reigning with Christ!  
*Verse: Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel (Ps. 67:36).*  
*Sticheron 2:*  
Having desired the fair beauty of the Bridegroom, Christ, O [Name], and wishing to be betrothed unto Him by all forms of good things, thou didst adorn thyself with the pains of abstinence: therefore now thou reignest with Him in His bridal chamber!  
*Verse: Precious in the sight of the Lord is the death of His venerable ones (Ps. 115:6).*  
*Sticheron 3:*  
Striving toward the divine haven, thou quietly passedst over the waves of worldly turmoils: and navigating the ship of thy soul unimmersed from the bitterness of pleasures, O all-honorable one, thou wast filled with secret possessions!  
*Glory... Tone 6:*  
Today thy sacred solemnity has shone forth brighter than the sun, illuminating souls that are in darkness, ever driving away demonic gloom, O wonderfully esteemed one!  
*Both now... Theotokion:*  
My Creator and Deliverer...[^484]  
*And if thou celebratest not, speak this Theotokion:*  
O Mother of God, thou art the true vine that didst bud forth for us the Fruit of life: we pray thee, intercede, O Lady, together with the holy Apostles, that He have mercy upon our souls!  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
Beholding Thee crucified, O Christ, she who gave Thee birth cried aloud: What strange mystery is this which I behold, O my Son? How dost Thou die in the flesh, suspended upon a tree, O Giver of life?  
*Troparion in the Typikon.*  
*And if there be no Typikon, speak this Troparion, Tone 8:*  
In thee, O mother, was preserved that which is according to the image, with exactness: for having taken up the cross, thou followedst Christ; and doing, thou didst teach to look beyond the flesh, for it passes away, and to care for the soul, a thing immortal: therefore also thy spirit rejoices together with the angels, O venerable [Name]!  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion, according to the tone of the Troparion.*  
*At Matins, at God is the Lord, the same Troparion.*  
*After the 1st Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 5.*  
*Tone 5, Podoben: "The Word Who is unoriginate with the Father and the Spirit" (Собезнача́лное сло́во):*[^485]  
Having manfully endured struggles through abstinence, thou didst overthrow the crafty adversary; and having lived a life in endurance, having piously run the course, O [Name], thou hast now passed unto God, praying for all who sacredly celebrate thy holy festival! *(Twice)*  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
All-immaculate Mother, shine forth upon me the beam of repentance, and dispel the gloom of mine immeasurable evils, drive away the wicked thoughts of my heart, O Virgin!  
*After the 2nd Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 4.*  
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Speedily anticipate" (Ско́рѡ предварѝ):*[^486]  
Thou didst crucify the flesh with its passions, and hadst all thy love toward Christ, O [Name], Thine incorruptible Bridegroom: therefore thou didst receive the crown, and wast numbered among the angelic choirs, O venerable one, praying fervently unto Him for those who honor thee! *(Twice)*  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
The tempest of sin troubles me, and the disturbance of disorderly thoughts; take pity, O all-immaculate one, and stretch forth a helping hand unto me, as thou art merciful, that being saved I may magnify thee, being saved!
*At the Polyeleos, Megalynarion:*  
We bless thee, O venerable mother [Name], and we honor thy holy memory: for thou prayest for us unto Christ our God!  
*Selected Psalm: With patience I waited for the Lord (Ps. 39:2).*  
*After the Polyeleos, Sessional Hymn, Tone 8.*  
*Tone 8, Podoben: "That which was secretly commanded" (Повелѣ́нное та́йнѡ):*[^487]  
Foreseen by God, O mother, being guided unto the haven of life, without turmoil thou didst pass through the tempest of life, hymning the Deliverer with the angels, and praying unceasingly for us, that Christ may grant us grace and mercy, preserving the flock which thou didst gather with labor! *(Twice)*  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
Rejoice, thou who from the Angel didst receive the joy of the world! Rejoice, thou who gavest birth unto thy Creator and Lord! Rejoice, thou who wast accounted worthy to be the Mother of God!  
*Gradual Hymns, the 1st Antiphon of Tone 4. Prokeimenon:*  
Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel (Ps. 67:36).  
*Verse: In the churches bless ye God, the Lord from the fountains of Israel (Ps. 67:27).*  
*Let every breath praise the Lord.*  
*Gospel from Matthew, Pericope 104 (Matt. 25:1–13).*[^488]  
*After Psalm 50, Sticheron, Tone 2:*  
The snaring of the soul and the carnal passions thou didst cut down with the sword of abstinence, and mental sins thou didst choke by the silence of fasting; and with the streams of thy tears thou didst water the whole wilderness, and didst cause the fruits of repentance to bud forth for us: therefore we celebrate thy memory, O venerable one!  
*If thou celebratest with Polyeleos:*  
*Canon of the Mother of God on 4, and of the venerable woman on 8.*  
*Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^489]  
The wondrously working rod of Moses, striking the sea in the sign of the cross and dividing it, overwhelmed the charioteer Pharaoh of old; but it saved the fugitive, foot-travelling Israel, as he sang a hymn unto God!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Being wholly submerged in the storms of passions, and my soul being tossed by the surging tempest of thoughts, guide me by thy prayers unto the calm haven of Christ's will, O venerable [Name], that I may worthily hymn thee!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Enlightened by divine virtues, O divinely blessed [Name], thou wast betrothed unto the pure Word, having lived divinely by fasting; and thou didst follow His life-bearing footsteps, having endured the mortification of passions!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Thou didst follow the instruction of the divine Fathers, O glorious [Name], and purely emulated their manner of life by abstinence, as one bodiless; and having lived in purity, virginity, and true humility, O venerable one!  
*Theotokion:*  
The divine jar and table that bare the Bread of life, the untilled field, the holy mountain, the Virgin Mother of God do we glorify with hymns!  
*Ode 3. Heirmos:*[^490]  
O Lord, Maker of the vault of heaven and Builder of the Church: do Thou establish me in Thy love, O summit of desire, foundation of the faithful, O only Lover of mankind!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Thou didst make thine abode in the light-radiant heavenly bridal chambers, having become a chosen bride unto the Almighty; thou pourest forth rivers of healings, O [Name], drying up the streams of our passions!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Adorned with extreme desire, longing for Christ Who through the abundance of tender-heartedness impoverished Himself in the flesh, thou didst follow His life-bearing words, having hated all the fair things of life having hated all the fair things of life!
*Troparion 3:*  
Having acquired the gilded wings of virtue, thou didst fly up unto the heavenly height, O blessed [Name], like an incorruptible dove!  
*Theotokion:*  
Thou didst save the human race, O Virgin, by the grace of Him Who ineffably impoverished Himself in the flesh from thy womb: therefore we honor thee, and piously call thee blessed, O all-pure Virgin full of the grace of God!  
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4.*  
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Thou Who wast lifted up" (Вознесы́йсѧ на крⷭ҇тъ):*[^491]  
Thou wast shown to be adorned with virgin beauties, through the sacred struggles of asceticism, as an immaculate bride and virgin of Christ; and with Him thou didst enter into the incorruptible bridal chamber, and dost enjoy His beauties: but pray that we may be saved from every distress, who hymn thee with love!  
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
Pure, all-immaculate, and unwedded one, who alone didst give birth in time unto the timeless Son and Word of God: pray Him together with the holy and honorable Apostles, Martyrs, Prophets, and Venerables, to grant us cleansing and great mercy!  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
O all-immaculate Virgin, Mother of Christ God, a sword pierced thy most holy soul when thou beheldest Thy Son and God willingly crucified: Whom pray unceasingly, O blessed one, to grant us forgiveness of transgressions!  
*Ode 4. Heirmos:*[^492]  
I have heard, O Lord, the mystery of Thy dispensation, I understood Thy works, and I glorified Thy Godhead!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Thy radiant solemnity, shining with the light of God's Spirit, enlightens the souls of us who with faith hymn thee, O venerable [Name]!  
*Troparion 2:*  
By divine miracles thou didst convince those who knew not God, and returnedst them unto the knowledge of Him, who left behind the darkness of deception!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Thou didst bring gifts unto Christ: the pure mortification of members, and the labors of abstinence; and in return thou didst receive the heavenly Kingdom, O [Name], and eternal delight!  
*Theotokion:*  
Having humbled me, save me who live in high-mindedness, O all-pure one who gavest birth unto Him Who lifted up our humbled nature!  
*Ode 5. Heirmos:*[^493]  
Waking early we cry unto Thee: O Lord, save us: for Thou art our God, besides Thee we know none other!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Thy hands venerably lifted up unto the Creator, O mother [Name], cast down the insolence of the enemy!  
*Troparion 2:*  
The Most High, having upheld thy right hand, guided thee, O mother, unto His heavenly delight!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Having passed through the narrow path in fasting, thou didst run unto the breadth of Paradise, O all-honorable [Name]!  
*Theotokion:*  
Those who know thee not as Mother of God, O Mother of God, shall not see the Light that was born of thee, O all-pure one!  
*Ode 6. Heirmos:*[^494]  
Grant me a robe of light, O Thou Who clothest Thyself with light as with a garment, O greatly merciful Christ our God!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Having mortified bodily movements, thou didst greatly reign over passions, and now, O all-honorable [Name], thou dwellest in passionless splendors!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Thou didst learn to bow down before the Savior's icon, O glorious one, by thy deeds and words, observing His saving teachings observing His saving teachings!
*Troparion 3:*  
Christ showed thee forth, O mother [Name], as a cloud that pours down the living rain unto those who ask of thee with faith, O most glorious one!  
*Theotokion:*  
Fairer than all sons of men is Thy Son, O all-pure one, in the beauty of the Godhead, even though He became flesh for our sake!  
*Kontakion in the Typikon.*  
*And if there be no Typikon, speak this Kontakion, Tone 2.*  
*Tone 2, Podoben: "Having received grace" (Бл҃года́ть прїе́мше):*[^495]  
For the love of the Lord, O venerable one, thou didst hate the desire for rest, having enlightened thy spirit through fasting: for thou stoutly conqueredst the beasts; but by thy prayers destroy the insolence of the adversaries!  
*Ikos:*  
Grant me a stream of the word, O my God, and make my mind a fountain of good order, and bless my tongue, that I may hymn Thine ewe-lamb, whom Thou hast crowned by Thy goodness: for unless Thou Thyself give me a worthy word, how shall I, being poor, bring a gift rich in words and deeds? Therefore grant me strength to declare her labors: for stoutly she conquered the beasts; but by her prayers destroy the insolence of the adversaries!  
*Ode 7. Heirmos:*[^496]  
The Hebrew children in the furnace boldly trampled upon the flame, and turned the fire into dew, crying: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God, unto ages!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Thou caredst not for decaying beauty, O venerable [Name], beholding the recompenses and light that are there, and the unaging glory and beauty of God, and the divine habitation!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Thou didst exchange the corruptible world for transcendent, unaging life; and temporary food for the abiding Godhead; and a carnal betrothed for the heavenly Bridegroom!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Thou didst acquire a God-loving mind, O [Name], and in the flesh wast likened unto the angels, keeping vigil with fervent love, and singing: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God, unto ages!  
*Theotokion:*  
Behold my affliction, O Virgin, which the multitude of mine evils has brought upon me, and snatch me from kinship with the fire, who cry aloud: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God, unto ages!  
*Ode 8. Heirmos:*[^497]  
The God-proclaiming children in the furnace, trampling the flame with fire, sang: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Adorned with the radiance of purity and enlightened by a pure life, O mother, thou standest before Thy Bridegroom Christ our God!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Thy holy body, O venerable one, which is most gloriously hidden in the grave, heals diverse sicknesses of men and drives away demonic malice!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Thou didst offer thyself as a sacred sacrifice and radiant offering, O venerable mother [Name], like fragrant incense unto the Master of all, Christ our God!  
*Theotokion:*  
Without corruption, beyond word, thou gavest birth unto the Word Who delivers from all corruption: therefore with faith we magnify thee, O Virgin!  
*Ode 9. Heirmos:*[^498]  
Every ear was terrified at the ineffable condescension of God, how the Most High willingly came down even unto the flesh, becoming man from a virgin womb: therefore we the faithful magnify the all-pure Mother of God!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Thou didst desire the noetic beauty of the Bridegroom, and purely lovedst Him: Where restest Thou? Where feedest Thou? thou didst fervently cry: May I rest with Thee and delight in Thy splendors, magnifying Thy goodness!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Thou hadst understanding in thy soul, and humility, and divine meekness, unhesitating faith, and hope and love toward God, cleaving unto Him with all-night prayers: and thou art enlightened and illumined with the splendors that are there, O blessed [Name]!
*Troparion 3:*  
The people have assembled today to magnify with praises the Lord Who magnified this Thy holy festival, wherein do thou, who standest before Christ Thy Bridegroom, O venerable and all-honorable [Name], remember us who remember thee!  
*Theotokion:*  
Spare me, O God Who wast born of the Virgin, and Who didst preserve incorrupt after childbearing her that bare Thee, when Thou shalt sit to judge my deeds: overlook my lawlessness and sins, forasmuch as Thou art sinless, a merciful God, and the Lover of mankind!  
*Exapostilarion:*[^499]  
Hiding from the prince's shame, and in a guise of wisdom she showed him foolish: being a virgin in soul and body, thou wast a man in understanding and faith, O venerable [Name], the beauty of virgins, the adornment of monastics!  
*Theotokion:*  
Make the day of spiritual joy shine upon me: for thou, O pure one, art the light and life of those who are in darkness, for thou art able to do what thou desirest, since thou art Sovereign Lady of all: deliver all from perils, and all who mourn from the snares of the crafty one!  
*If thou celebratest with the Polyeleos: At the Praises, Stichera, Tone 4.*  
*Tone 4, Podoben: "As one valiant" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́блѧ):*[^500]  
Thou didst nobly subject the carnal stirrings unto the soul by ascetic labors, and didst follow Christ, and didst dwell in all reverence among the ascetics, O [Name], quenching the fiery passions with divine showers of tears, and kindling a most fervent longing for the Creator!  
*Sticheron 2:*  
Thou didst raise up a sacred dwelling for the benefit of many unto God, having become a temple of the Holy Spirit, O all-wise one, through purity of soul; and thou art a guide of souls unto profitable ascetic labors, and didst bring these forth as a saved dowry unto the Master, O [Name]: with whom we honor thee with faith!  
*Sticheron 3:*  
The maidens, submitting themselves unto thy teaching, loved the Bridegroom and Lord: and scorning carnal sweetness in deed, they piously restrained their passions with zeal, and were led with thee, O [Name], into the heavenly and divine bridal chamber, rejoicing evermore!  
*Glory, Tone 8:*  
O marvelous wonder! How zealously thou didst offer thyself unto God through ascetic afflictions; and by ascetic tears thou didst fulfill divine love, and didst conquer carnal passions, and didst trample down demons by self-restraint; and thou becamest a bride of the Almighty!  
*Now and ever: Theotokion:*  
Taking up the voice of the Archangel Gabriel, let us say: Rejoice, O Mother of God, who gavest birth unto Christ the Giver of life for the world!  
*At the Liturgy: the Beatitudes from the Canon, Ode 3 and Ode 6.*  
*Prokeimenon, Tone 4:* God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^501]  
*Verse:* In the congregations bless ye God, the Lord from the fountains of Israel.*[^502]  
*Epistle to the Galatians, pericope 250.*[^503]  
*Alleluia, Tone 1:* I waited patiently for the Lord, and He inclined unto me, and heard my prayer.*[^504]  
*Gospel from Matthew, pericope 104, or of Luke, pericope 33.*[^505]  
*Communion Hymn:* The righteous shall be in everlasting remembrance.*[^506]

## Common Service to Several Venerable Women (Two or Many)

*At Vespers, at "Lord, I have cried," Stichera, Tone 1.*  
*Tone 1, Podoben: "Joy of the heavenly hosts" (Небе́сныхъ чинѡ́въ):*[^507]  
Shining from above like God-radiant rays, ye enlighten the world with spiritual beams: ye drive away darkness, and the crafty legions of demons: therefore we celebrate your radiant and divine festival.  
Having beheld paradise's beauties, and having been richly nourished, O all-honorable ones, ye budded forth unto the world the incorrupt flowers of divine understanding: partaking whereof today with spiritual love, we work every good fruit of soul.  
By monastic life ye adorned the ascetic multitudes, having shone forth like the sun unto all: therefore with them ye are glorified, O most honorable ones, delighting in divine glory, and praying that we may be saved.  
*Glory, now and ever: Theotokion:*  
Tempest-tossed by the abyss of transgressions, having fled unto the calm harbor of thy most pure prayers, O Mother of God, I cry unto thee: Save me, stretching forth thy mighty right hand unto thy servant, O all-immaculate one!  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
Lo now, I have seen my heart pierced with a spear, my Child, even as they foretold unto me of old, when I beheld Thee, Who art fairer than all sons of earth: for upon the Cross Thou wast willingly suspended with thieves like an evildoer, O Christ!  
*If thou celebratest with the Polyeleos: At "Lord, I have cried," Stichera on 6.*  
*Glory, Tone 8:*  
By outpourings of tears ye quenched the burning of carnal pleasures; and having kindled the desire of divine longing, ye were able to be united without passion unto Christ the King: therefore now ye have entered understandingly into the bridal chamber, to pray unto the Lover for those who honor you.  
*Now and ever: Theotokion:*  
*The King of heaven, for His love toward mankind...*[^508]  
*Entrance, Prokeimenon of the day, and 3 Readings:*  
*Look in the Service for Martyrs.*[^509] *Litiya, on the reverse.*[^510]  
*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 4.*  
*Tone 4, Podoben: "As one valiant" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́блѧ):*[^511]  
Having journeyed by the narrow way, sailing with an untroubled spirit unto the life that leads thither: for ye were the boast of ascetics, O venerable ones, and stoutly cast down demonic snares; therefore, having attained to be inheritors of the heavenly kingdom, ye delight in unceasing beauty.  
*Verse:* God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^512]  
Your most radiant life astonished the angels, and greatly terrified the demonic insolence: and it brightly adorned the assemblies of the faithful, commanding them ever to journey unto Christ's heavenly dwelling; Whom do ye entreat, that they who with faith celebrate your all-honorable memory may be delivered from corruption and perils.  
*Verse:* Precious in the sight of the Lord is the death of His venerable ones.*[^513]  
Offering hymnody and praise unto Christ every day, O venerable ones, dwelling in the wilderness, having committed souls and thoughts unto the only God, like the great Moses ye entered into unapproachable places: therefore ye conquered the invisible enemy, and became a pure receptacle of the Divine Spirit.  
*Glory, Tone 4:*  
The maidens, having loved the Bridegroom and Lord, and submitting themselves unto His teaching, and scorning carnal weakness from themselves, piously restrained their passions with zeal, and were led with you into the heavenly bridal chamber, O venerable ones, rejoicing evermore!
*Now and ever: Theotokion:*  
*Look upon the petitions of thy servants...*[^514]  
*And if thou celebratest not, speak this Theotokion:*  
O heavenly candle, righteous throne, all-pure Mother of God, pray that our souls may be saved!  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
Weep not for Me, O Mother, beholding hanging upon the Tree Thy Son and God, Who uncontainably suspended the earth upon the waters, and created all creation: for I shall arise and be glorified, and I shall crush Hades' kingdoms with might, and destroy his power, and deliver the captives from his malice, forasmuch as I am compassionate, and have brought them unto My Father, as Lover of mankind!  
*Troparion in the Typikon.*  
*And if there be no Typikon, speak this Troparion, Tone 2:*  
Betrothed unto true desire, O Christ-renowned ones, and having renounced union with a temporal betrothed, and grown through virtuous labors, ye ascended unto the height of incorruption, O noble-souled and most rich ones, pillars and rule of monastic women: therefore entreat Christ God unceasingly for us who celebrate your memory with love!  
*Glory, now and ever: Theotokion according to the tone of the troparion.*  
*At Matins, at "God is the Lord," the same Troparion.*  
*After the first Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 3.*  
*Tone 3, Podoben: "The beauty of virginity" (Красотѣ̀ дѣ́вства):*[^515]  
Ye left worldly beauty, O venerable ones, having exchanged corruptible wealth for that which is truly impassable and abiding, O holy ones: therefore with all the saints we celebrate your holy and glorious memory, asking to find great mercy through your prayers, O blessed ones! *(Twice)*  
*Glory, now and ever: Theotokion:*  
Inconceivable and incomprehensible, O Mother of God, full of the grace of God, is the fearful mystery of God accomplished in thee: for having conceived the Uncontainable One, thou barest Him, clad in flesh from thy most pure blood; Whom do thou ever entreat as thy Son, O pure one, that our souls may be saved!  
*After the second Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 5.*  
*Tone 5, Podoben: "The Word Who is unoriginate with the Father and the Spirit" (Собезнача́лное сло́во):*[^516]  
Having adorned your life with self-restraint and mortified your members, ye conquered the enemy's assaults, O venerable ones; and dwellers in the wilderness and spiritual luminaries unto the world ye appeared, O blessed ones: therefore pray unto the Lord, that He have mercy upon our souls! *(Twice)*  
*Glory, now and ever: Theotokion:*  
Having found thee to be a harbor and a wall, a refuge, hope, shelter, and fervent intercessor, O faithful, we flee unto thee, and cry diligently, and call aloud with faith: Have mercy, O Mother of God, upon those who hope in thee, and deliver us from transgressions!  
*At the Polyeleos, Megalynarion:*  
We bless you, O venerable mothers, and we honor your holy memory: for ye pray for us unto Christ our God!  
*Selected Psalm:*  
*I waited patiently for the Lord...*[^517]  
*After the Polyeleos, Sessional Hymn, Tone 8.*  
*Tone 8, Podoben: "That which was secretly commanded" (Повелѣ́нное та́йнѡ):*[^518]  
Keeping vigil in secret prayers, and having delighted in the God-inspired Scriptures, O blessed ones, they took the Lord's Cross upon their shoulders; and following Him in self-restraint, they cast down all the serpent's deceit, crying unto Christ: O heavenly Bridegroom, be Thou be Thou our confirmation! *(Twice)*
*Glory, now and ever: Theotokion:*  
O Good One, Who for our sake wast born of the Virgin, and didst endure crucifixion, Who by death didst overthrow death, and manifested the Resurrection as God: despise not those whom Thou hast fashioned with Thy hand; show forth Thy love for mankind, O Merciful One; accept the Mother of God who bare Thee, as she intercedes for us; and save, O our Savior, a despairing people![^519]  
*Gradual, the first Antiphon of Tone 4.*  
*Prokeimenon, Tone 4:* God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^520]  
*Verse:* In the congregations bless ye God, the Lord from the fountains of Israel.*[^521]  
*Let every breath praise the Lord.*  
*Gospel from Matthew, pericope 104.*[^522]  
*After Psalm 50, Sticheron, Tone 8:*  
By outpourings of tears ye quenched the burning of carnal pleasures; and having kindled the desire of divine longing, ye loved Christ the King, to be united without passion unto Him: therefore now ye have entered understandingly into the bridal chamber, to pray unto the Lover for those who honor you!  
*If thou celebratest with the Polyeleos:*  
*Canon of the Mother of God on 4, and of the Venerable Women on 8.*  
*Tone 8, Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^523]  
The charioteer Pharaoh did the wonderworking staff of Moses once drown of old, striking crosswise and dividing the sea; but it saved the fleeing people of Israel as they traveled on foot, chanting a song unto God.  
*Troparion 1:*  
Through carnal weakness ye cast down the strong enemy, and ye ever draw near unto God, O venerable women, praying that He grant saving strength unto us all!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Ye were wounded by the Bridegroom's beauty, and after Him ye clave according to the Psalmist, following His life-bearing footprints: and therefore ye cast down the enemy serpent!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Having withered carnal beauties with ascetic labors, ye adorned your souls: and with Christ the Bridegroom ye entered into radiant bridal chambers, O venerable ones!  
*Theotokion:*  
Thy womb was shown forth as a light-resembling bridal chamber, O Virgin, for the sake of mercy, of the Lord Who was bodily incarnate of thee, and Who enlightened all things with the beams of the knowledge of God!  
*Ode 3. Heirmos:*[^524]  
O Lord, Maker of the vault of heaven and Builder of the Church: do Thou establish me in Thy love, O summit of desires, confirmation of the faithful, O only Lover of mankind!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Ye despised bodily beauties, O most honorable ones, and accounted fleeting glory as a dream: but in humility and fasting ye sought God, O God-blessed ones!  
*Troparion 2:*  
The sacred relics of the venerable women shine forth, granting healing unto those who flee unto them with faith, having drawn grace from saving fountains, having emulated His voluntary passions!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Ye renounced the world and carnal wisdom, and by self-restraint and labors acquired Christ, the most pure Bridegroom, Who grants unto you the heavenly bridal chamber of divine delight!  
*Theotokion:*  
The sacred trumpets of the sacred prophets of old proclaimed thee, who gavest birth unto the Door of light, and an animate scroll, wherein the Word was written, not with hands, beyond word, O all-pure Virgin!  
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4:*  
Having taken up Christ's Cross upon your shoulders, ye followed Him with ascetic deeds in true faith, O all-radiant and venerable ones; and unto all women practicing asceticism ye purely appeared as an all-honorable rule: but by divine labors ye inherited the kingdom on high; entreat unceasingly, that our souls may be saved our souls!
*Glory, now and ever: Theotokion:*  
We have known the Father's Word, Christ our God, Who was incarnate of thee, O Virgin Mother of God, only pure one, only blessed one: therefore unceasingly hymning thee, we magnify thee!  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
She who in the latter times bare in the flesh Him Who was begotten of the unoriginate Father, beholding Thee hanging upon the Cross, O Christ, cried aloud: Woe is me, O most beloved Jesus! How art Thou, Who art glorified as God by angels, now willingly crucified by lawless men, O Son? I hymn Thee, O Long-suffering One!  
*Ode 4. Heirmos:*[^525]  
I have heard, O Lord, the mystery of Thy dispensation, I understood Thy works, and I glorified Thy Godhead.  
*Troparion 1:*  
The venerable women behold the all-pure beauty of their beloved Bridegroom, having withered carnal beauty through self-restraint.  
*Troparion 2:*  
Having cast off slumber from the eyelids of the soul, O sacred women, ye put to sleep bodily movements through self-restraint.  
*Troparion 3:*  
Your radiant festival, shining with the light of God the Spirit, enlightens our souls, who honor you with faith, O venerable ones!  
*Theotokion:*  
Having loved Thee, O Lord, Who wast incarnate of the most pure Virgin, the venerable ones ran after the fragrance of Thy myrrh, being wounded by Thy divine love!  
*Ode 5. Heirmos:*[^526]  
Why hast Thou cast me off from Thy presence, O never-setting Light, and an alien darkness has covered me, the wretched one? But turn me back, and unto the light of Thy commandments direct my paths, I pray Thee!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Loosing all infirmities by the power of the Holy Spirit, ye drive away painful gloom with the marvelous beams of your wonders; and unto the great light of the heavenly kingdom ye guide the faithful, O all-honorable ones!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Having died unto the world, ye inherited immortal life, and were counted worthy of the divine bridal chamber, O God-blessed ones, having preserved your lamps unquenched with the ascetic oil, O all-honorable ones!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Scorning fleeting and decaying life, and leaving it unto those upon earth, ye bound yourselves with spiritual love, O blessed ones: therefore now ye dwell where the habitations of the righteous are!  
*Theotokion:*  
God the Word of God willingly took up His abode in an unwedded womb, and was seen as man, Who preserved thee as a virgin, O all-pure one: Whom the maidens desiring, followed Him in asceticism by a radiant life!  
*Ode 6. Heirmos:*[^527]  
I shall pour out my prayer unto the Lord, and unto Him shall I declare my griefs, for my soul is filled with evils, and my life has drawn near unto Hades; and like Jonah I pray: Lead me up from corruption, O God!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Having acquired self-restraint and a humble heart, vigilance with almsgiving, understanding and faith, and perfect love, O sacred women, ye became a temple of God, and an inexhaustible fountain of wonders!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Having estranged yourselves from worldly pleasures through the endurance of ascetic pains, O honorable ones, for the sake of Him Who came down upon earth for our sake and willingly sojourned, on this account ye acquired in heaven Him Who welcomed you as guests, O marvelously honorable ones!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Ye put to sleep soul-destroying passions with many vigils, O all-honorable ones: and ye worthily fell asleep with the sleep of the righteous, for praying for the world!
*Theotokion:*  
I know thee to be the intercessor of life and a steadfast guardian, O Virgin, who dost resolve the supplications of misfortunes and drive away the assaults of demons: and I pray ever, that thou deliver me from the corruption of my passions!  
*Kontakion in the Typikon.*  
*And if there be no Typikon, speak this Kontakion, Tone 2:*  
Having worn out your bodies with fasting, and entreating the Creator with watchful prayers for your sins, that ye might receive perfect forgiveness; and having received divine remission and the heavenly kingdom: entreat Christ God for us all!  
*Ikos:*  
O Thou Who shuttest the abyss and openest it; Who liftest up water in the clouds and givest rain upon the face of the earth: do Thou, O God, grant unto my barren soul well-ordered speech for the opening of my lips, and clear utterance unto a stammering tongue, that I may worthily hymn Thy venerable ones, whom Thou Thyself hast glorified: for for Thy sake they despised the fair things of this world, and wore out their flesh, and by Thy might conquered the devil, and received from Thee crowns of endurance; and now, standing in the heavens with all the saints, they entreat unceasingly for us all!  
*Ode 7. Heirmos:*[^528]  
The children who came down from Judea in Babylon once trampled upon the furnace's flame by faith in the Trinity, chanting: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou!  
*Troparion 1:*  
Having through love exchanged decaying glory for incorruptible glory, O venerable ones, whereof enjoying evermore, rejoicing and making merry they chant: Blessed is the God of our fathers!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Having appeared as many-lighted stars, enlightening the souls of the faithful with the light of your labors, ye teach them to chant: Blessed is the God of our fathers!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Ye appeared as a river of healing, drowning the sea of passions of those who strive to be saved: Blessed is the God of our fathers!  
*Theotokion:*  
Deliver my wretched soul from the bonds of sin, O Mother of God, and yoke me unto the perfect love of God, that with faith I may glorify thee and chant unto ages!  
*Ode 8. Heirmos:*[^529]  
Having become conquerors of the tyrant and the flame by Thy grace, strictly attending unto Thy commandments, the children cried: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!  
*Troparion 1:*  
By reason, O virgins, ye subjected senseless passions, and unto the Word the Bridegroom ye were spiritually united, chanting: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto ages!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Having been crucified unto the world, and wounded by divine love, O sacred virgins, ye wounded with the arrows of self-restraint him who wounded Eve by a sweet taste, singing praise unto Christ unto ages!  
*Troparion 3:*  
By mind, O venerable ones, ye conquered foolish desires, and unto the Word the Bridegroom ye were betrothed without blemish, O honorable ones, chanting: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto ages!  
*Theotokion:*  
Having given birth unto Jesus incarnate from thy pure blood, O all-pure Virgin Mother, thou hast gathered all virgins together, crying with them: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto ages!  
*Ode 9. Heirmos:*[^530]  
Truly we confess thee to be the Mother of God, who are saved by thee, O pure Virgin, magnifying thee with the bodiless choirs.  
*Troparion 1:*  
Fair ewe-lambs, and God-proclaiming turtledoves, and swallows and lovers of purity, ye were brought unto the heavenly beauties.
*Troparion 2:*  
Ye were united unto the supreme chorus, and settled in the assemblies of the elect, praying with love unto the merciful God for us.  
*Troparion 3:*  
The memory of the venerable women has shone forth upon the world like the sun, enlightening the minds of the faithful who ever magnify Christ.  
*Theotokion:*  
O sacred receptacle of Light, enlighten my soul darkened by passions, O Virgin, and deliver me from the outer darkness by thy supplications!  
*Exapostilarion:*[^531]  
Deliver from every peril through your prayers unto God our Deliverer, O venerable and all-honorable ones, those who celebrate your prayerful memory with love; and cleanse the filth of the soul of me who have hymned you in small measure!  
*Theotokion:*  
Cease not, O Virgin, to pray unto Thy Son, God the Lover of mankind, for us: for in thee have we acquired our hope, and by thy protection we are delivered from perils, and passions, and transgressions, and sicknesses, who glorify thee with faith!  
*If thou celebratest with the Polyeleos: At the Praises, Stichera, Tone 4.*  
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Thou hast given a sign" (Да́лъ є҆сѝ зна́менїе):*[^532]  
Having preserved your virginity incorrupt, O venerable ones, having lived blamelessly and purely, and received wealth, ye fulfilled Christ's legislation; Whom ye followed, leaving the earth and what is on it: therefore Jesus, Lover of mankind and Savior of our souls, granted you heavenly things and heaven! *(Twice)*  
*Sticheron 2:*  
Ye philosophically showed forth the soul's nature to be self-moving and immortal: therefore ye also easily took care to abide pure from the filth of sin and every mire, having the bond of the flesh without single filth or blemish, O God-welcoming women, but pure and spotless ye stood before your Bridegroom!  
*Sticheron 3:*  
Your light-resembling faces were illuminated with radiance, and having died, O all-praised ones, unto Christ Who glorified you as His own God-beholding venerable ones, as merciful ones, as God-lovers and God-proclaiming ones: for ye lived an angel-like life upon earth, and pleased God by the brilliance of your life!  
*Glory, Tone 1:*  
Having put off this bodily weight, O venerable women, by self-restraint and afflictions ye ascended unto the heavenly mansions, wherein ye delight in the ineffable beauty beloved by you!  
*Now and ever: Theotokion:*  
Rejoice from us, O holy Virgin Mother of God, pure vessel of all the universe, unquenchable lamp, receptacle of the Uncontainable One, invincible temple! Rejoice, from whom was born the Lamb of God, Who takes away the sins of the whole world!  
*At the Liturgy: the Beatitudes from the Canon, Ode 3 and Ode 6.*  
*Prokeimenon, Tone 4:* God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^533]  
*Verse:* In the congregations bless ye God, the Lord from the fountains of Israel.*[^534]  
*Epistle to the Galatians, pericope 250.*[^535]  
*Alleluia, Tone 1:* I waited patiently for the Lord, and He inclined unto me, and heard my prayer.*[^536]  
*Gospel from Matthew, pericope 104, or of Luke, pericope 33.*[^537]  
*Communion Hymn:* Rejoice in the Lord, O ye righteous.*[^538]

## Common Service to a Single Venerable-Martyr Woman

*At Vespers, at "Lord, I have cried," Stichera, Tone 8.*  
*Tone 8, Podoben: "What shall we call you" (Что́ вы нарече́мъ):*[^539]  
O martyr, all-praised passion-bearer [Name],[^540] having cast down carnal passions by self-restraint, by suffering thou didst put to death the enemy serpent; having astonished the angels by thy pains, having gladdened men by thy sufferings: adornment of monastics, vessel of virginity, pray that our souls may be saved and enlightened!  
What shall we call thee, O glorious one? A bride of Christ who blossomed in the beauties of virginity; a chosen daughter of the Jerusalem on high; a fellow-inhabitant and converser with angels, who delightest in the spiritual bridal chamber: O [Name], much-suffering one, adornment of monastics, pray that our souls may be saved and enlightened!  
Thou didst endure the uprooting of teeth, the severing of hands and feet and breasts, being cut limb from limb by lawless torturers, gazing unto the Bridegroom's beauty, and enduring pains beyond nature, O [Name], much-suffering one, incorrupt bride of Christ: pray that our souls may be saved and enlightened!  
*Glory, now and ever: Theotokion:*  
What is more marvelous than thy beauties, O all-pure one? Thou healest the sick, and deliverest from passions, and drivest away the assaults of weaponless adversaries; thou deliverest from perils those who hymn thee, and allayest affliction by thy prayer: be an intercessor with beams of joy unto thy servants, O Virgin, praying that our souls may be saved!  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
When the ewe-lamb beheld her Lamb willingly stretched upon the Tree of the Cross, she cried aloud maternally, suffering with weeping: O my Son, what is this strange vision? Giving life unto all as Lord, how art Thou put to death, O Long-suffering One, granting resurrection unto the earthly? I glorify Thy great condescension, O my God!  
*If thou celebratest with the Polyeleos: At "Lord, I have cried," Stichera on 6.*  
*Glory, Tone 2:*  
With voices of rejoicing and in acclamations with psalms, let us hymn the venerable-martyr [Name]: for she cast down idolatrous deceit unto the earth, and manfully put the adversary enemy beneath her feet; therefore having finished her course she flew up unto the heavens, bearing a crown upon her head, and crying: Unto Thee, my Bridegroom, do I desire, and having love of desire, for Thy sake I surrendered my flesh unto torments, that I may dwell in the honorable habitations, where the dwelling of those who rejoice is!  
*Now and ever: Theotokion:*  
*The shadow of the law has passed away, now that grace has come...*[^541]  
*Entrance, Prokeimenon of the day, and 3 Readings:*  
*Look in the Service for a Woman-Martyr, fol. 45 on the reverse.*[^542]  
*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 4.*  
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Thou hast given a sign" (Да́лъ є҆сѝ зна́менїе):*[^543]  
Thou didst endure suffering, twofold by thy contest, O all-praised [Name], having mingled the blood of martyrdom with the sweats of self-restraint, O venerable one: therefore also the Benefactor granted thee a twofold crown, unto Whom thou didst ascend brightly adorned, as an all-immaculate virgin, and as an invincible martyr!

*Verse:* God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^544]  
The bodily beauty and the divine comeliness of thy soul concurred together: like a lily in the habitations of the venerable ones thou didst bloom, adorned with flowers, and reddened with blood-streams, O all-immaculate bride [Name]: therefore also the fair heavenly bridal chamber received thee into an indestructible bridal chamber, as a virgin and martyr!  
*Verse:* In the congregations bless ye God, the Lord from the fountains of Israel.*[^545]  
Round about thee came the Angel who delivers thee, O all-praised [Name]: for from infancy thou wast shown forth fearing the Lord, and offered as an honorable offering, preserved for the Almighty: therefore thou stoutly trampledst down the tyrant's godlessness, and ascendedst unto Christ Thy Bridegroom!  
*Glory, Tone 6:*  
At the right hand of the Savior thou didst stand, O virgin passion-bearer and martyr [Name], clad in virtues, unconquered, and adorned with the oil of purity and the blood of suffering, and crying unto Him, holding a lamp with joy: Unto the fragrance of Thy myrrh I ran, O Christ God, for I am wounded by Thy love; separate me not, O heavenly Bridegroom! By her prayers send down Thy mercies upon us, O all-powerful Savior!  
*Now and ever: Theotokion:*  
*My Creator and Deliverer...*[^546]  
*And if thou celebratest not, speak this Theotokion:*  
The eye of my heart I lift up unto thee, O Sovereign Lady: despise not my small sighing in the hour when thy Son shall judge the world; be thou my shelter and helper!  
*Cross-Theotokion:*  
Beholding Thee crucified, O Christ, she that bare Thee cried aloud: What strange mystery is this which I behold, O my Son? How upon the Tree dost Thou die suspended in the flesh, O Giver of life?  
*Troparion in the Typikon.*  
*And if there be no Typikon, speak this Troparion, Tone 4:*  
Thy lamb [Name], O Jesus, cries with a great voice: Thee, my Bridegroom, do I love, and seeking Thee I endure suffering, and am crucified and buried with Thy baptism, and suffer for Thy sake, that I may reign in Thee, and die for Thee, that I may also live with Thee: but as a spotless sacrifice accept me, who have sacrificed myself unto Thee with love: by her prayers, forasmuch as Thou art merciful, save our souls!  
*Glory, now and ever: Theotokion according to the tone of the troparion.*  
*At Matins, at "God is the Lord," the same Troparion.*  
*After the first Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 8.*  
*Tone 8, Podoben: "The Wisdom and Word" (Премꙋ́дрости):*[^547]  
With the dew of self-restraint thou didst youthfully extinguish the flame of passions, and with the fire of thy blood didst burn up all deceit; and unto the Word the Bridegroom thou didst bring as a gift honorable virginity and valiant suffering: therefore into the bridal chamber of glory He led thee, who didst brightly strive and trample down the serpent, O [Name], much-suffering one: entreat Christ God, that He grant remission of sins unto those who honor thy holy memory with love! *(Twice)*  
*Glory, now and ever: Theotokion:*  
As a Virgin and only one among women, who didst seedlessly give birth unto God in the flesh, all generations of men bless thee: for the fire of the Godhead dwelt in thee, and like an infant thou didst suckle the Creator and Lord: therefore we, the angelic and human race, worthily glorify thine all-holy birthgiving, and harmoniously cry unto thee: Entreat Christ God, that He grant remission of transgressions unto those who worthily chant thy glory!
*After the second Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 1.*  
*Tone 1, Podoben: "Thy tomb, O Savior" (Гро́бъ тво́й Сп҃се):*[^548]  
First fasting, and secondly the blood of martyrdom, like an alabaster box of myrrh for love thou didst bring unto Christ thy Bridegroom; and reward thou didst receive from Him, O marvelously honorable martyr [Name]: a divine and incorruptible crown, and grace of healings through spiritual power! *(Twice)*  
*Glory, now and ever: Theotokion:*  
O unwedded pure Virgin Mother of God, only protection and shelter of the faithful: deliver all from perils, and grievous afflictions, and surrounding circumstances, who have their hope in thee, O Maiden, and save our souls by thy divine supplications!  
*At the Polyeleos, Megalynarion:*  
We magnify thee, O holy passion-bearer [Name], and we honor thy holy memory: for thou prayest for us unto Christ our God!  
*Selected Psalm:*  
*God is our refuge and strength...*[^549]  
*After the Polyeleos, Sessional Hymn, Tone 8.*  
*Tone 8, Podoben: "The Wisdom and Word" (Премꙋ́дрости):*[^550]  
Having bound thy soul with the love of Christ, corruptible and temporal things, O wise martyr, thou didst pass by with oblivion, as a glorious disciple of the Word: having first mortified passions in asceticism, secondly in suffering thou didst put the deceiver to shame: therefore also of twofold boldness before the Creator thou wast counted worthy, O venerable one, having glorified Him, O [Name], most rich one: entreat Christ God, that He grant remission of transgressions unto those who honor thy holy memory with love! *(Twice)*  
*Glory, now and ever: Theotokion:*  
As the all-immaculate bride of the Creator, as the unwedded Mother of the Deliverer, being the receptacle of the Comforter, O all-hymned one: hasten to deliver me from their wickedness, who am a foul dwelling of lawlessness, and in understanding have become a plaything of demons: having made me a radiant dwelling through virtues, O light-giving and incorrupt one, drive away the cloud of passions, and count me worthy of the communion on high, and of the unwaning Light by thy prayers!  
*Gradual, the first Antiphon of Tone 4.*  
*Prokeimenon, Tone 4:* God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^551]  
*Verse:* In the congregations bless ye God, the Lord from the fountains of Israel.*[^552]  
*Let every breath praise the Lord.*  
*Gospel from Matthew, pericope 104.*[^553]  
*After Psalm 50, Sticheron, Tone 6:*  
*At the right hand of the Savior thou didst stand, O virgin...*[^554]  
*Written at the Aposticha, Glory.*  
*If thou celebratest with the Polyeleos:*  
*Canon of the Mother of God on 4, and of the Venerable-Martyr on 8.*  
*Tone 8, Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^555]  
Let us sing unto the Lord, Who led His people through the Red Sea, for He alone is gloriously glorified.  
*Troparion 1:*  
Unto those who honor this thy light-bearing memory, O God-wise one, grant me enlightenment, driving away the darkness of my soul!  
*Troparion 2:*  
Wholly from swaddling clothes thou didst offer thyself unto thy Creator, O [Name], and with the fire of self-restraint didst burn up carnal passions!  
*Troparion 3:*  
Unto the height of martyrdom thou didst ascend, O martyr, not having spared the flesh; and of spiritual bridal chambers as a virgin thou wast counted worthy!  
*Theotokion:*  
Thee, the unburnt ladder whereon God was established, having made men heavenly, O Maiden, we honor!

*Common Service to a Venerable-Martyr Woman*
*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
Thou art the confirmation of those that run unto Thee, O Lord; Thou art the light of the darkened, and my spirit praises Thee.*[^556]
*Troparion 1:*
Thou didst stand before the tribunal of the tormentor, O glorious [Name], preaching Christ, the Creator and Master of all, God the Word!
*Troparion 2:*
The inward beauty of heart having manifested itself in visible appearance, O glorious [Name], revealed thee as most fair unto those who beheld thee.
*Troparion 3:*
An inexhaustible wealth of healings Christ bestowed upon thee, O maiden, Whose poverty thou didst willingly love.
*Theotokion:*
Consume the material substance of my sins with the fire of thy prayers, O Mother of Light, bearing unto me the divine dew of forgiveness!
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4:*[^557]
As a noetic ewe-lamb, thou didst offer thyself unto the Lamb and Shepherd by fasting; and having finished the course by martyrdom and kept the faith, O [Name], therefore today with joy we celebrate thy holy memory, O marvelous one, glorifying Christ.
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Receive the prayers of thy servants, O Mother of God, and deliver us from every distress, as having given birth unto Christ the Savior, the Deliverer of our souls.
*Stavrotheotokion:*
Possessing thy protection, O all-pure one, and being delivered from tribulations by thy prayers, preserved everywhere by the Cross of thy Son, as is due, we all piously magnify thee.

*Common Service to a Venerable-Martyr Woman*
*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
I have heard, O Lord, the mystery of Thy dispensation; I considered Thy works, and glorified Thy Divinity.*[^558]
*Troparion 1:*
The most light yoke of Christ having borne from youth, O martyr, to bear a heavy burden the most lawless condemned thee.
*Troparion 2:*
The drops of thy blood extinguished the coals of polytheism, and the rays of thy miracles, O [Name], burned up the material substance of the passions.
*Troparion 3:*
Raising their fire above the ground, O martyr, they kindled it against thy breast, inflaming thy longing which was toward the Master, O virgin.
*Theotokion:*
After giving birth thou didst remain incorrupt, O Virgin, even as before birthgiving; for thou didst bear as an infant God Who is known before the ages.
*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
Rising early, we cry unto Thee: O Lord, save us; for Thou art our God, besides Thee we know none other.*[^559]
*Troparion 1:*
Thou didst appear unwasted by material fire, O martyr [Name], for the inward fire of divine love bedewed thee.
*Troparion 2:*
Adorned with the buffetings of thy face, O martyr [Name], thou didst smite the abominable folly of the enemies.
*Troparion 3:*
Stretched upon the tree, O God-wise [Name], through love for Thy Bridegroom thou didst prefigure the divine Passion.
*Theotokion:*
We hymn thee, O all-hymned Sovereign Lady, Mother of God; for the much-hymned God in the flesh thou didst bear, O all-pure one.
*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
Grant me a radiant robe, Thou Who clothest Thyself with light as with a garment, O greatly merciful Christ our God.*[^560]
*Troparion 1:*
While thy body was being broken with wounds, the uprightness of thy conscience *Common Service to a Venerable-Martyr Woman*
...toward Christ our God was made manifest, O passion-bearer [Name].
*Troparion 2:*
Hanging on high, enduring wounds, O martyr, and invulnerably preserving the nobility of soul, O [Name], worthy of all praise!
*Troparion 3:*
The venerable one endured the cutting off of her breasts, O glorious one, nourishing the faithful with the milk of divine love.
*Theotokion:*
Having given birth unto God, the Lover of mankind, O God-blessed Sovereign Lady: pray Thou unto Him that we may be delivered from the fire of Gehenna!
*Kontakion, Tone 4. Podoben: "Speedily anticipate" (Ско́рѡ предварѝ):*[^561]
Thy divine memory, O [Name], having shone forth today, hath appeared unto the world as the sun, recounting thy life; for by self-restraint thou didst destroy carnal impulses, and by the blood of suffering didst betroth thyself unto Christ. Therefore deliver from all evils those who praise thee, that we may cry unto thee: Rejoice, O venerable mother!
*Ikos:*
Open my lips, O most blessed one, by thy God-pleasing prayers, standing before God, O all-glorious venerable-martyr, that I may sing of thy divine life and worthily tell of thy sufferings which upon earth with fervent love thou didst accomplish, and wast an unconquerable martyr! By faith thou didst direct the vigil of self-restraint and didst love purity. Therefore deliver from all evils those who praise thee, that we may cry unto thee: Rejoice, O venerable mother!
*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
The Hebrew children in the furnace boldly trampled upon the flame, and turned the fire into dew, crying: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God, unto ages!*[^562]
*Troparion 1:*
Where the sound of those keeping festival is, there dost thou dance, O passion-bearer [Name], singing with the virgins unto God the Creator: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God, unto ages!

*Common Service to a Venerable-Martyr Woman*
*Troparion 2:*
Beholding thy limbs being broken, O [Name], and enduring the tearing out of thy nails, thou didst offer thyself as a sacrifice unto God, singing: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God, unto ages!
*Troparion 3:*
Thou didst appear as a God-wise vine, O [Name], having thy hands and feet pruned like branches, and pouring out for us spiritual wine that makes glad hearts and drives away the drunkenness of passions.
*Theotokion:*
He that is undefiled clothed Himself with the whole man, without sin; He came forth bearing flesh from thy womb, O pure one: entreat Him to save those who honor thee in faith.
*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
The Chaldean tyrant furiously kindled the furnace sevenfold for the godly; but seeing them saved by a higher power, he cried out unto the Creator and Deliverer: Ye children, bless; ye priests, sing praises; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages!*[^563]
*Troparion 1:*
Having valiantly struggled in youthful courage, O all-lauded passion-bearer, thou didst raise trophies of victory over deceit; for thou didst endure the deprivation of hands and feet, and the rooting out of thy breasts and teeth, O martyr, thou didst endure, singing in joy: Ye people, supremely exalt Christ unto the ages!
*Troparion 2:*
Thou didst shine forth as the sun with the brightness of virginity, and didst gleam with the beauties of martyrdom, and didst enlighten the world with the radiant beams of thy endurance, O passion-bearer, crying: Ye children, bless; ye priests, sing praises; ye people, supremely exalt Christ unto the ages!
*Troparion 3:*
My soul defiled with passions, O bride of God, and blackened by the assaults of the serpent, cleanse from fierce ills by thy supplications, and with thy bright vesture, O martyr [Name], illumine it to cry: Ye priests, sing praises; ye people, supremely exalt Christ unto the ages!
*Theotokion:*

*Common Service to a Venerable-Martyr Woman*
Desiring thee, the pure and all-immaculate one, the virgin preserved both body and soul blameless; having reduced to ashes the fire of passions through endurance, and having endured the testing of many torments, now in the heavenly bridal chambers she rejoices with thee, being glad unto all ages.
*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
Heaven was astounded at this, and the ends of the earth were amazed, that God appeared in the flesh unto men, and thy womb became more spacious than the heavens; therefore the hierarchies of angels and men magnify thee as the Mother of God.*[^564]
*Troparion 1:*
Thou wast suspended upon the tree, prefiguring the blessed Passion of the Word of God; and thou didst endure the cutting off of thy hands and feet, the pulling out of thy teeth, and the severing of thy tongue and breasts, O pure [Name], glory of monastics and ornament of martyrs.
*Troparion 2:*
As a bride thou wast adorned with beauty, having betrothed thyself unto the Bridegroom by benefic suffering; and within the radiant chambers thou didst take up thine abode as a chosen one, carrying a virgins lamp; and now thou reignest, shining with those that live forever, O [Name].
*Troparion 3:*
Thy pains drop down sweetness that takes away the bitterness of sin; and thy shrine pours forth rivers of healings, and drowns all passions and cruel diseases, unto the glory of the Savior, O precious [Name], by those who worthily glorify thee.
*Theotokion:*
Knowing Thee, O Lord, Who didst bear flesh from a woman, Thy martyr, adorned with virgins rays and crimsoned with the blood of martyrdom unto Thee, O God, was gloriously led in the train of Thy Mother, unto Thee Who rulest over creation.
*Exapostilarion:*[^565]
Thou pourest forth healings like a river unto those who run with faith unto thy precious shrine, O God-like [Name], vessel of virginity *Common Service to a Venerable-Martyr Woman*
...and fair flower of nature, daughter of the King, possessing within the blessedness of divine glory.
*Theotokion:*
Having given birth unto the hypostatic Wisdom, the pre-eternal Word, and the Physician of souls and bodies, heal the suppurations and wounds of my soul, grievous and temporal, and calm the pain of my heart, O Virgin.
*If thou celebratest with the Polyeleos: At the Praises, Stichera, Tone 8. Podoben: "O most marvelous wonder" (Ѡ҆ пресла́внагѡ чꙋдесѐ):*[^566]
Forsaking the beauty of the world, thou didst illumine the nobility of soul with the beauties of nobility from on high, O all-lauded [Name]!
