Having preserved unspotted by thy life the grace of the Image, O unconquerable martyr, God-like bulwark of virginity, and most noble flower of nature! *(Twice)*
Having adorned thy soul with word, and life, and grace, and endurance, thou didst lead armies of sufferers and a choir of virgins unto Him Who shone forth from the Virgin, and Who openly opened the entrance of testimony unto all, O all-wise [Name]; with them now entreat Him to save thy flock.
The Builder of righteousness, as a pure virgin and as a glorious martyr, divinely adorned thee with double crowns, having granted thee a fair chamber enlightened with radiant manifestations, wherein having made thine abode, O bride of Christ, thou hast now manifestly been enriched with eternal blessedness.
*Glory... Tone 4. Idiomelon:*
The bright beauty of thy soul appeared more radiant than the morning-radiance of thy body, O wise and most wealthy venerable-martyr, beauty of female ascetics, boast of passion-bearers, ever-flowing fountain of miracles, expeller of unclean spirits, and intercessor for those who honor thy memory!

*Common Service to a Venerable-Martyr Woman*
*Both now... Theotokion:*
We have acquired thee as a rampart, O all-pure Mother of God, and a calm haven and confirmation; therefore I pray, who am storm-tossed in life: pilot and save me!
*At the Liturgy, the Beatitudes from the Canon, Odes 3 and 6.*
*Prokeimenon, Tone 4:* God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^567]
*Verse:* In the congregations bless ye God, the Lord from the fountains of Israel.*[^568]
*Epistle to the Corinthians, pericope 181*[^569] *or to the Galatians, pericope 250.*[^570] *Alleluia, Tone 1:*
*Verse:* With patience I waited for the Lord, and He was mindful of me, and gave ear unto my supplication.*[^571]
*Gospel from Matthew, pericope 62*[^572] *or Mark, pericope 21.*[^573]
*Communion Verse:* The righteous shall be in everlasting remembrance.*[^574]

## Common Service to a Hieroconfessor and a Venerable Confessor

*At Vespers, on "Lord, I have cried", Stichera, Tone 4. Podoben: "As one valiant among martyrs" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́блѧ):*[^575]
We know thee as the establishment of truth, the confirmation of the Faith, and the expounder of dogmas, harmony of piety, dwelling-place of purity, and a chosen receptacle; fragrance of the Spirit, and a great treasure of teachings, and foundation of the Church of Christ, O divinely inspired sacred manifestation, [Name]!
We praise thee as successor of the Apostles, peer in character of the sufferers, fellow-zealot of the ascetics; seal of teachers and divine representation; mystagogue of Christ, O all-lauded God-bearer, and God-flowing river of understandings, drowning the thoughts of the lawless and of blasphemers, O all-wise [Name]!

*Common Service to a Hieroconfessor and a Venerable Confessor*
Having multiplied the talent of wisdom, O most wealthy one, thou wast counted worthy of the joy of thy Lord; variegated with the grace of divine brilliance, and shining spiritually with noetic dawn, thou dost now stand at the life-bearing right hand, ever illumined thence with rays, O glorious [Name]!
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Wash away the filth of my passionate heart, O all-hymned Mother of God, and cleanse all its wounds and suppurations which come from sin, O pure one; and stay the instability of my mind, that I may magnify thy power and great protection, wretched and unprofitable servant that I am!
*Stavrotheotokion:*
Beholding Christ, the Lover of mankind, being crucified and pierced in the ribs with a spear, the all-pure one wept, crying out: What is this, O my Son? What have the ungrateful people rendered unto Thee in place of the good things Thou didst do for them, and strivest to make me childless, O most dearly beloved? I marvel, O compassionate One, at Thy voluntary crucifixion!
*If thou celebratest with the Polyeleos: On "Lord, I have cried", Stichera on 6.*
*Glory... Tone 6:*
Grace was poured out upon thy lips, O venerable father [Name], and thou becamest a shepherd of the Church of Christ, teaching the noetic sheep to believe in the consubstantial Trinity in one Godhead!
*Both now... Theotokion:*
*Who will not call thee blessed, O most holy Virgin...*[^576]
*Entrance. Prokeimenon of the day, and three readings (see in the Service to a Venerable, fol. 4v).*[^577]
*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 8. Podoben: "O most marvelous wonder" (Ѡ҆ пресла́внагѡ чꙋдесѐ):*[^578]
O all-wise father [Name], thou didst clarify the Church of Christ with hymns, divine *Common Service to a Hieroconfessor and a Venerable Confessor*
...by the operation of the Spirit, striking thy psaltery, imitating David in God-likeness; proclaiming this with divine songs, thou didst draw all men unto thyself.
*Verse: Precious in the sight of the Lord is the death of His saints.*[^579]
O all-glorious father [Name], having withdrawn from the turbulent clamor of the world, thou didst run unto the calm quiet of Christ, O venerable one; and in the visions of divine deeds, with all-wealthy brightnesses manifestly thou wast truly enriched, and didst transmit them unto the faithful, through a divinely comely life, O most blessed one!
*Verse: Blessed is the man that fears the Lord; in His commandments he will desire exceedingly.*[^580]
Come, ye earthborn, let us piously hymn today with songs the sacred and honorable solemnity of the venerable one, [Name]; for behold, truly he was counted worthy to receive the light of divine radiance! O the unutterable compassion of Thy heart, O Master and Lord, whereby we have come to know how to glorify Thee, the All-good One!
*Dismissal Hymn, Tone 8:*[^581]
The multitudes of monastics honor thee as teacher, O our father [Name]; for by thy path we have learned truly to walk uprightly! Blessed art thou having labored for Christ, and thou didst expose the power of the enemy, O conversationalist with angels, companion of the venerable and the righteous; with them pray thou unto the Lord, that our souls may find mercy!
*Both now... Theotokion:*
*O unwedded Virgin...*
*If thou dost not celebrate, say this Theotokion:*
I, O holy Virgin Mother of God, flee unto thy protection, knowing that through thee I shall find salvation; for thou art able, O pure one, to help me.
*Stavrotheotokion:*
The undefiled heifer, seeing the Bullock nailed upon the tree of His own will, weeping sorrowfully, cried out: Woe is me, O most beloved Child! What hath the ungrateful Hebrew gathering rendered unto Thee, desiring to bereave me of Thee, O most dearly beloved?

*Common Service to a Hieroconfessor and a Venerable Confessor*
*Troparion in the Typikon.*
*If there is no Typikon, say this Troparion, Tone 8:*[^582]
Guide of Orthodoxy, teacher of piety and purity, luminary of the universe, divinely inspired adornment of hierarchs, O all-wise [Name]; by thy teachings thou hast enlightened all, O spiritual flute: entreat Christ God that our souls may be saved!
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion according to the tone of the Troparion.*
*At Matins, at "God is the Lord", the same Troparion.*
*After the first Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 4. Podoben: "Thou Who wast lifted up upon the Cross" (Вознесы́йся на крⷭ҇тъ):*[^583]
Having disregarded the enjoyment of earthly and corruptible things, and the beauty of the world, and temporal food, thou didst love life in the wilderness, whence thou wast counted worthy to be united with the choir of martyrs and ascetics; with them pray to save thy servants! *(Twice)*
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
O pure, all-immaculate, and unwedded one, who alone didst bear in time the timeless Son and Word of God: entreat Him with the holy and honorable apostles, martyrs, prophets, and the venerable, to grant us cleansing and great mercy!
*After the second Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 4. Podoben: "Speedily anticipate" (Ско́рѡ предварѝ):*[^584]
Having followed Christ, thou didst forsake the world, and having visibly subdued the flesh by self-restraint, O most blessed [Name], whence *Common Service to a Hieroconfessor and a Venerable Confessor*
...therefore thou didst possess the anointing of the priesthood; and now thou hast passed over unto the incorporeal choirs, praying for all of us who praise thee. *(Twice)*
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
O thou who alone didst bear the Creator of all, who alone didst adorn mankind by thy birthgiving, O all-pure one: deliver me from the snares of craft Belial; establish me upon the rock of Christs desires, diligently entreating Him Whom thou didst incarnate.
*At the Polyeleos, Megalynarion for a Hieroconfessor:*
We magnify thee, O Hieroconfessor of Christ, [Name], and we honor Thy holy memory; for thou prayest for us unto Christ our God.
*Selected Psalm: Hear these things, all ye nations...*[^585]
*For a Venerable Confessor, the Megalynarion:*
We bless thee, O Venerable Confessor of Christ, [Name], and we honor thy holy memory, teacher of monastics and companion of angels.
*Selected Psalm: With patience I waited for the Lord...*[^586]
*After the Polyeleos, Sessional Hymn, Tone 3. Podoben: "Of the divine faith" (Бж҃е́ственныѧ вѣ́ры):*[^587]
Enlightened by the Holy Spirit, thou didst proclaim with boldness the tradition of Orthodoxy, O all-blessed [Name], and didst put to shame the lawless tormentor when unjustly sent into exile, O venerable father! Entreat Christ God to grant remission of sins unto those who honor thy holy memory with love. *(Twice)*
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Whithersoever anyone is being saved, thither he rightly flees; and what other refuge is such as thee, O Mother of God, who coverest our souls?

*Common Service to a Hieroconfessor and a Venerable Confessor*
*Gradual, the first Antiphon of Tone 4.*
*Prokeimenon, Tone 4:* Precious in the sight of the Lord is the death of His saints.*[^588]
*Verse:* What shall I render unto the Lord for all that He hath rendered unto me?*[^589]
*Let every breath praise the Lord.*
*Gospel from Luke, pericope 64.*[^590]
*After Psalm 50, Sticheron, Tone 6:*
O venerable father, into all the earth went forth the report of thy right deeds, therefore in the heavens thou hast found the reward of thy labors; thou didst destroy the legions of demons and attain unto the ranks of angels, whose life thou didst blamelessly emulate: having boldness before Christ God, ask peace for our souls!
*If thou celebratest with the Polyeleos: Canon of the Mother of God on 4, and of the Confessor on 8.*
*Tone 6, Ode 1. Heirmos:*
Having traversed the deep on foot as on dry land, Israel, seeing the persecutor Pharaoh drowned, cried out: Let us sing a song of victory unto God!*[^591]
*Troparion 1:*
With whole mind the sacred [Name] loved Thee, O Savior, applying himself firmly unto suffering, enduring afflictions and sicknesses and struggles, until he found endless food.
*Troparion 2:*
With sprinklings of blood, O glorious one, thou didst calm the sea of unbelief; thou wast a river of Christ, truly watering the Church continually.
*Troparion 3:*
Having offered thy far-extended struggles upon earth unto God, O glorious [Name], Thou wast counted worthy to receive the crowns of the heavenly Kingdom and indestructible eternal life.
*Theotokion:*
Adorned with the beauty of virtues, O pure Mother of God, thou didst conceive the Virtue of the true God; us with divine *Common Service to a Hieroconfessor and a Venerable Confessor*
...Who hath enlightened us with divine virtues.
*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
There is none holy like Thee, O Lord my God, Who hast exalted the horn of Thy faithful, O Good One, and established us upon the rock of Thy confession.*[^592]
*Troparion 1:*
Thou gavest neither slumber to thine eyelids nor sleep to thine eyes, O venerable father [Name], until thou didst reveal thyself as a dwelling-place of the Holy Trinity and a treasure of wisdom, enriching the world with golden-rayed teachings, O blessed one!
*Troparion 2:*
Having become a keeper of the divine commandments, O God-wise [Name], Thou wast shut up in prisons, opening unto the faithful the barrier of the pedagogical path that leads into the breadth of understanding.
*Troparion 3:*
Strengthened by divine power, thou didst cast down the powerless audacity of the lawless by steadfast confession, O all-sacred [Name], sufferer, as a soldier of the divine host!
*Theotokion:*
The Wisdom and Word of the Father, Who existed before all ages, having ineffably become incarnate in the last times of thee, the unwedded Mother, made thee the Mother of God.
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4. Podoben: "Thou hast appeared today" (Ꙗ҆ви́лся є҆сѝ):*[^593]
As a glorious sun, as the morning star on high, Christ set thee over His Church, enlightening the faithful with thy words, O heavenly initiate of mysteries, [Name], our God-wise father!
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
O pure, all-immaculate, and unwedded one, who alone didst bear in time the timeless Son and Word of God: entreat Him with the holy and honorable apostles, martyrs, prophets, and the venerable, to grant us cleansing and great mercy!

*Common Service to a Hieroconfessor and a Venerable Confessor*
*Stavrotheotokion:*
When Thine all-pure Mother beheld Thee lifted up upon the Cross, O Word of God, weeping maternally, she said: What is this new and strange wonder, O my Son? How dost Thou, the Life of all, approach death, desiring to make alive those that are dead, as Thou art compassionate?
*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
Christ is my power, God and Lord, the venerable Church divinely sings, crying out from a pure mind, keeping festival in the Lord.*[^594]
*Troparion 1:*
With the spear of thy words thou didst pierce godlessness, and by thy sufferings as with a sword thou didst cut down the demonic hosts; from whose malice deliver those who honor thee, O all-lauded [Name]!
*Troparion 2:*
Not sacrificing to demons, nor fearing death, nor the reality of tortures, the hieromartyr [Name] cried out: I confess the one God known in the Trinity!
*Troparion 3:*
The Lord, seeing the all-glorious height of thy humility, from on high bestows upon thee the word of dispensation, whereby the fierce elevations of heresies were brought low, O father!
*Theotokion:*
As the gate leading unto the divine entrance, as the divine paradise, as the noetic place of radiance, as the beauty of Jacob, we bless the Virgin!
*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
With Thy divine light, O Good One, illumine with love the souls of those that rise early unto Thee, I pray, that they may know Thee, O Word of God, the true God, Who callest them from the darkness of sin.*[^595]
*Troparion 1:*
By the flow of thy tears, O all-wise father [Name], thou didst dry up the abyss of voluptuous pleasures; and by the outpouring of thy teachings, O blessed one, thou didst dam up the torrents of heresies!
*Troparion 2:*
Thou wast a spacious church truly for the Trinity, O father [Name], adorned with grace, and with the illumination of honorable virtues, and with instructional humility.

*Common Service to a Hieroconfessor and a Venerable Confessor*
Having cast down by thy patience all the insolence of the evil one, deliver me from his injury, making me vigilant in every way by paths leading unto God, O glorious [Name]!
*Theotokion:*
We sing of Him, O all-pure one, Who for our sake took of thee passible and mortal flesh, and visibly was born in a blended, unconfused hypostasis.
*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
Beholding the sea of life surging with the tempest of temptations, fleeing unto Thy calm haven, I cry unto Thee: Lead up my life from corruption, O greatly Merciful One!*[^596]
*Troparion 1:*
Having become a luminary, enlightening the ends of the earth with the light of thy sufferings, thou didst confess the Name of Christ before tormentors, O all-lauded passion-bearer of the Lord, [Name]!
*Troparion 2:*
Thy tongue truly became as the pen of a swiftly writing scribe, confessing divine understanding, inscripting on the tablets of hearts the spiritual law, O father [Name]!
*Troparion 3:*
Those sailing into the calm of thy teaching, O divinely speaking father, are delivered from the soul-harming abyss of turmoil, and those coming from other heresies are saved through faith.
*Theotokion:*
The Holy Spirit, having come upon thee, O pure one, guardian of virginity, made thee an honorable dwelling-place, O all-immaculate and all-blessed one, for the Son of the Most High.
*Kontakion in the Typikon.*
*If there is no Typikon, say this Kontakion, Tone 2:*[^597]
Having taken delight in self-restraint, O God-wise one, and having put to sleep carnal desires, thou didst appear nurtured by faith, and didst blossom forth like the paradise tree of life, O [Name], sacred father!

*Common Service to a Hieroconfessor and a Venerable Confessor*
*Ikos:*
Open my lips, O most sacred one, by thy God-pleasing prayers, standing before God, O all-glorious venerable-martyr, that I may sing of thy blessed life and worthily tell of thy virtues which upon earth with fervent love thou didst accomplish; for thou wast a steadfast confessor, having directed self-restraint by faith, and didst love vigil and purity, O [Name], sacred father!
*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
The Angel made the furnace dew-dropping for the venerable children, but the command of God, consuming the Chaldeans, persuaded the tyrant to cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!*[^598]
*Troparion 1:*
As thy life was most radiant because of fasting, O God-wise one, so thy suffering appeared steadfast; for thou didst manifestly glorify Christ, crying: Blessed is the God of our fathers!
*Troparion 2:*
Girded with the courage of purity of chastity, O father pleasing unto God, [Name], and having received through grace a radiant crown of martyrdom, thou didst cry: Blessed is the God of our fathers!
*Troparion 3:*
The impious who did not bow down before Thine all-pure icon, O Savior, grievously afflicted Thy servant, who ever spoke and cried unto Thee: Blessed is the God of our fathers!
*Theotokion:*
He that sits uncircumscribed in the bosom of the Father, O all-pure one, having now proceeded from thy womb, sits circumscribed, conforming Himself unto thy likeness, in order to save Adam, having become Adam.
*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
From the flame Thou didst pour forth dew for the venerable ones, and the sacrifice of the righteous man Thou didst consume with water; for Thou doest all things, O Christ, only by willing them: Thee we supremely exalt unto all ages!*[^599]
*Troparion 1:*
Luminously thou didst show thyself, like another sun *Common Service to a Hieroconfessor and a Venerable Confessor*
...sending forth teaching unto the ends of the earth, taking away the darkness of ignorance of every sin, O blessed [Name], by the light of repentance.
*Troparion 2:*

*Troparion 3:*
Thou wast adorned, O all-blessed one, with the beauty of thy words; for thou didst appear as the pen of the Holy Spirit, writing piously for the faithful divine understanding, supremely exalting Christ unto the ages.
*Theotokion:*
I flee unto thy protection, O all-immaculate one, and now present thee as intercessor for my life, who beyond understanding didst bear God the Word: Whom we supremely exalt unto all ages.
*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
It is not possible for men to see God, upon Whom the ranks of angels dare not gaze; but through thee, O all-pure one, the incarnate Word appeared unto men: Whom magnifying, with the heavenly hosts, we bless thee!*[^600]
*Troparion 1:*
As light, as the moon, as a great sun, as lightning having shone forth upon the Church of Christ, Thou didst enlighten the minds of the faithful; having made thine abode in the unwaning light, singing with tri-hypostatic voices unto the uncreated Trinity.
*Troparion 2:*
The earth was enriched by thy holy tomb, O [Name], and by thy sacred body, O all-lauded one; and the spirits of the righteous leaped for joy; and angels, O blessed one, received thy spirit: with them remember all of us who sing of thee.
*Troparion 3:*
Having become a temple of the All-holy Spirit, and a river filled with living waters, and an immovable foundation of the Church, degree of Orthodoxy, ever-flowing stream, worthy of marvel fountain of divine repentance, O [Name]!
*Theotokion:*
As a heavenly drop the rain descended into thy womb, O Virgin *Common Service to a Hieroconfessor and a Venerable Confessor*
...and dried up the deceitful streams, a fountain of incorruption and deliverance unto all men through thee, O woman full of the grace of God!
*Exapostilarion:*[^601]
Having first been well tried by suffering, and secondly having accomplished the divine course by fasting, thou didst ascend into the heavens, and dost stand before Christ, O [Name], venerable father: fervently praying for us who sing of thee.
*Theotokion:*
Having given birth unto the ineffable Joy, the Lord, make us partakers of heavenly joy, all who honor thee, O all-pure one, and who offer thanksgiving unto thee from the soul: forget not thy servants, O blessed Mary!
*If thou celebratest with the Polyeleos: At the Praises, Stichera, Tone 4. Podoben: "Thou hast given a sign" (Да́лъ є҆сѝ зна́менїе):*[^602]
The grace of the Spirit having shone upon thee, O father [Name], brightly enlightened thee; therefore thou didst diminish the night of passions, and didst reach the day of dispassion: being pure, thou wast united unto the purest Light. Abiding wherein, forget not, O divinely speaking one, those who sing in faith of thy sacred memory!
Bearing the desired heavenly grace unto thee, O [Name], thou didst disregard earthly things. Therefore, as one incorporeal, thou didst choose a life of suffering: as desiring piously to take delight in the stream of everlasting sweetness, O acceptable unto Him! And with tears thou didst dry up the turbid fountain of passions, O venerable one, and didst water ears of corn that nourish souls.
Thou didst worthily receive the grace of healings, O [Name], from divine grace; therefore the deceit of evil spirits is cast down by thee, and of incomparable diseases; having fled corruption, thou didst pass over, O venerable one: for thou pourest forth like waters *Common Service to a Hieroconfessor and a Venerable Confessor*
...thy gracious streams, being watered wherefrom, we sing of thy memory.
*Glory... Tone 5:*
O venerable father, thou gavest neither sleep to thine eyes nor slumber to thine eyelids, until thou didst free soul and body from passions, and didst prepare thyself as a dwelling-place for the Spirit; for Christ with the Father came and made His abode with thee; and having become a servant of the consubstantial Trinity, O great preacher, our father [Name], pray thou for our souls!
*Both now... Theotokion:*
We bless thee, O Virgin Mother of God, and we the faithful glorify thee as is due: the unshakable city, the impregnable rampart, the firm protectress and refuge of our souls.
*At the Liturgy, the Beatitudes from the Canon, Odes 3 and 6.*
*Prokeimenon, Tone 7:* The venerable ones shall boast in glory, and shall rejoice upon their beds.*[^603]
*Verse:* Sing unto the Lord a new song; His praise is in the church of the venerable ones.*[^604]
*Epistle to the Ephesians, pericope 233.*[^605]
*Alleluia, Tone 6:*
*Verse:* Blessed is the man that fears the Lord; in His commandments he will desire exceedingly.*[^606]
*Verse:* His seed shall be mighty upon the earth.*[^607]
*Gospel from Luke, pericope 64.*[^608]
*Communion Verse:* The righteous shall be in everlasting remembrance.*[^609]

## Common Service to Unmercenaries and Wonderworkers

*At Vespers, on "Lord, I have cried", Stichera, Tone 4. Podoben: "Thou hast given a sign" (Да́лъ є҆сѝ зна́менїе):*[^610]
Being flooded like rivers with spiritual waters, and manifestly filled, ye water creation with divine signs and marvelous gifts of healings; and ye dry up soul-destroying passions, and heal diseases, and drive away spirits, O God-bearing Unmercenaries, [Name and Name], intercessors for our souls!
Having subdued the irrational passions by spiritual powers, O holy ones, ye bestow beneficence upon men and beasts, having been enriched by Christ with the gift of healings; therefore, celebrating your sacred and light-bearing solemnity, we ask cleansing for our souls!
Your holy temple was shown forth as a light-bearing and saving heaven, having now acquired saving wonders like stars, and like the sun the radiant operation of divine healings, O most blessed [Name] and most glorious [Name], servants of the Lord and intercessors for our souls!
*If thou celebratest with the Polyeleos: On "Lord, I have cried", Stichera on 6.*
*Glory... Tone 8:*
Who will not marvel? Who will not glorify? Who will not faithfully sing of the wonders of the wise and all-glorious Unmercenaries? For even after their holy departure, they bestow abundant healing upon all who run to them with faith, and *Common Service to Unmercenaries*[^611]
...their honorable and holy relics pour forth the grace of healings! O holy pair! O honorable heads! O wisdom and glory! O grace given unto you from God! Therefore with songs we cry unto God the Benefactor, Who hath revealed you for the healing of our souls and bodies.
*Both now... Theotokion:*
*"The King of heaven, through love for mankind..."*[^612]
*Entrance. Prokeimenon of the day, and three readings (see in the Service to Martyrs, fol. 54v).*[^613]
*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 4. Podoben: "Of the heavenly hosts" (Нб҃ныхъ чинѡ́въ):*[^614]
Having shone upon all the inhabited earth with the rays of wonders, O holy Unmercenaries, most radiant luminaries, ye were likened unto heaven through grace, by the virtues of your lofty life, set forth as stars!
*Verse: For the saints that are in His earth, the Lord hath magnified all His desires in them.*[^615]
Surpassing rain-bearing clouds with the copious-flowing grace of your wonders, O martyrs of the Lord, ye spiritually water all the earth, compelling men to offer healthy hymns unto God!
*Verse: Behold now, what is so good or what is so pleasant, as for brethren to dwell together in unity?*[^616]
Having truly received skill from God to heal the passions of soul and body, O martyrs of the Lord, preternaturally ye heal all things, not by human medicinal remedies, but by divine inspirations!
*Glory... Tone 4:*
Having a fountain of healings, O holy Unmercenaries, [Names], ye bestow healings unto all that have need: as having been accounted worthy of the greatest gifts from the ever-flowing fountain of Christ the Savior. For the Lord said unto you, as unto peers of the Apostles: "Behold, I have given you power over unclean spirits, so as to cast them out, and to heal every disease and every infirmity."[^617] Therefore, having lived well according to His commandments, freely ye received, freely give, healing the passions of our souls and bodies!

*Common Service to Unmercenaries*
*Both now... Theotokion:*
*"Look down upon the entreaties of thy servants..."*[^618]
*If thou dost not celebrate, say this Theotokion:*
O Mother of God, Queen of all, boast of the Orthodox, tear down the pride of heretics, and put to shame their faces, who neither bow down nor honor thine honorable icon, O all-pure one!
*Stavrotheotokion:*
Beholding Thee crucified and pierced in the ribs with a spear, O all-pure one, Christ the Lover of mankind, she wept, crying out: What is this, O my Son? What have the ungrateful people rendered unto Thee in return for the good things that Thou didst do for them, and strivest Thou to leave me childless, O most beloved One? I marvel, O Compassionate One, at Thy voluntary crucifixion!
*Troparion in the Typikon.*
*If there is no Typikon, say this Troparion, Tone 8:*[^619]
Holy Unmercenaries and wonderworkers, visit our infirmities: freely ye received, freely give unto us!
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion according to the tone of the Troparion.*
*At Matins, at "God is the Lord", the same Troparion.*
*After the first Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 2:*[^620]
Having been worthy of so great gifts, O glorious ones, ye lived in a humble manner upon earth; and passing everywhere, freely healing the diseases and passions of the sick, ye were shown forth as companions of angels, [Names], faithful and all-comely brethren *Common Service to Unmercenaries*
...and heal our passions by your prayers! *(Twice)*
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Being a fountain of tender-heartedness, account us worthy of mercy, O Mother of God; look down upon a people that have sinned, show forth as always thy power: for putting our trust in thee, we cry unto thee "Rejoice!", as once did Gabriel, the supreme commander of the bodiless hosts!
*After the second Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 4:*[^621]
Passion-bearers of Christ, [Names], prayer for us who come unto you with faith: for by your prayers ye grant healing of good things, and drive away very many diseases, as protectors of our life! *(Twice)*
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
All-pure Mother of God, blessed in the heavens and glorified on earth, rejoice, unwedded Bride!
*At the Polyeleos, Megalynarion:*
We magnify you, O glorious wonderworkers, [Names], and we honor your honorable sufferings, which ye endured for Christ.
*Selected Psalm: God is our refuge and strength...*[^622]
*After the Polyeleos, Sessional Hymn, Tone 5. Podoben: "Co-beginningless" (Собезначáльное):*[^623]
Today the feast of the passion-bearers, [Names], hath shone forth, for it hath radiance from heaven: the angelic choir keeps festival, and mankind keeps feast together; therefore they pray for our souls! *(Twice)*
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
The mystery of the marvelous Virgin was shown forth as salvation to all the world, from whom Joy was born: O Lord, glory to Thee!
*Gradual, the first Antiphon of Tone 4.*
*Prokeimenon, Tone 4: For the saints that are in His earth, the Lord hath magnified all His desires in them.*[^624]
*Verse: I beheld the Lord ever before me...*[^625]

*Gospel from Mark, pericope 23.*[^626]
*After Psalm 50, Sticheron, Tone 1:*
The light-bearing, holy, and noble all-festal memory of the martyrs, [Names], which enlightens all the earth, driving away the gloom of sin, pours forth the grace of healing!
*If thou celebratest with the Polyeleos: Canon of the Mother of God on 4, and of the Unmercenaries on 8.*
*Tone 8, Ode 1. Heirmos:*
Unto Him Who shook off the tyranny of Pharaoh in the sea, and led Israel through dry land, let us sing unto Christ: for He is glorified unto ages of ages!*[^627]
*Troparion 1:*
Unto Him Who revealed unmercenaries and physicians in all the world, [Names], and who heal the diseases of all by grace, let us sing unto the Deliverer, Christ the Savior: for He is glorified unto ages of ages!
*Troparion 2:*
From spiritual treasures, the saints pour forth rivers of wonders and heal diseases: let us the faithful praise Him Who gave them this power: for He is glorified unto ages of ages!
*Troparion 3:*
Offer prayers unto God, O Unmercenary physicians, that He may deliver us from temptations, and many afflictions, and from the trembling and fearful torment of that day!
*Theotokion:*
Ever-present protectress of the afflicted, salvation and hope of the despairing, all-praised Mother of God, pray ever unto Christ, that we may be delivered from dangers!
*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
My heart is established in the Lord, my horn is exalted in my God; my mouth is enlarged over mine enemies, I have rejoiced in Thy salvation!*[^628]
*Troparion 1:*

*Troparion 3:*

*Theotokion:*

*Common Service to Unmercenaries*

Having trampled upon downward-dragging pleasures, O sufferers, ye were brightly lifted up unto divine height by grace, [Names], luminaries of the universe! Therefore we pray: deliver us from the gloom of sin and from diseases, praying unto God Who is over all!
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Thou art an unconquerable rampart for us Christians, O Virgin Mother of God; for fleeing unto thee, we remain unharmed, and having sinned again, we have thee as intercessor. Therefore giving thanks we cry unto thee: Rejoice, full of grace, the Lord is with thee!
*Stavrotheotokion:*
O Virgin all-immaculate, Mother of Christ God, a sword passed through thine all-holy soul when thou didst behold Thy Son and God voluntarily crucified: Whom, O blessed one, pray thou unceasingly to grant us forgiveness of transgressions!
*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
Mystically foreseeing Thee, O Word, incarnate of the Virgin, the prophet sang, crying out: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!*[^630]
*Troparion 1:*
Marvelous is the Savior, our God, for in the graves lie *Common Service to Unmercenaries*
...the bare bones of His saints, and they work marvelous and awesome wonders in the world: glory to Thy power, O Lord!
*Troparion 2:*
Having left earthly things as corruptible, ye became heirs of Zion, and worthy citizens of the Kingdom of Christ, O holy ones!
*Troparion 3:*
Worthily are ye praised upon earth, O unmercenary healers of the sick, [Names]; for even after your departure ye deliver all from diseases!

*Kontakion in the Typikon.*

Having received the grace of healings, ye extend health unto those in necessities, O physicians, all-glorious wonderworkers; but by your visitation cast down the audacity of warriors, healing the world with wonders!
*Ikos:*
The word of the wise physicians surpasses all understanding and wisdom, and bestows understanding unto all; for having received grace from the Most High, invisibly they grant health unto all. Whence unto my narrative also grace was granted to sing of the God-bearing servants and ministers of Christ, who bestow a multitude of healings; for they deliver all from illnesses, healing the world with wonders!

Physicians of the ailing, treasures of healings, saviors of the faithful, all-glorious Unmercenaries, heal those calling upon you in necessities and the sick, entreating the good God that we may be delivered from the snares of the enemy! *(Twice)*
The fountain of healings healed only one in a year; but the temple of the Unmercenaries heals the whole multitude of the ailing: for unfailing and inexhaustible is the wealth of the saints. By their prayers, O Christ, have mercy on us!
The choir of the saints rejoices unto ages, for they inherited the Kingdom of heaven; the earth having received their relics breathed forth sweet fragrance: servants of Christ were they, having taken up their abode in life eternal!
*Glory... Tone 6:*

*Common Service to Unmercenaries*

And their names, invoked with faith, heal incurable diseases. By them, O Lord, free us also from spiritual and bodily passions, as Thou art the Lover of mankind!
*Both now... Theotokion:*
Thou art the transformation of the sorrowful, the alleviation of the sick, O all-hymned Mother of God: save city and people, the reconciliation of the embattled, the calm of the storm-tossed, the only protectress of the faithful!

## Common Service to Fools for Christ's Sake[^644]

*At Vespers, on "Lord, I have cried", Stichera, Tone 8. Podoben: "O most marvelous wonder" (Ѽ пресла́внагѡ чꙋдесѐ):*[^645]
O blessed and God-wise [Name], thy pure soul, shining with orthodox understanding, radiant with the brightnesses of virtues, enlightens the fullness of the faithful, driving away demonic darkness; therefore as a partaker *Common Service to Fools for Christ's Sake*
...of the never-waning grace, all of us piously call thee blessed, O worthy of marvel!
Having a merciful soul, and a pure mind, and a vigilant heart, unbeguiled faith, and truly unhypocritical love, from earth thou hast departed unto the heavenly ones, and takest up thine abode with the choirs of the righteous; therefore we honor thee with sacred songs, and piously all of us glorify thee, O worthy of marvel, [Name]!
Strengthened by divine teachings, thou didst drive away carnal passions from thy soul, and wast a most purified dwelling-place for the Holy Trinity; therefore thou didst pass over unto divine life, while God even now preserves thy blessed body unharmed, by divine judgment, which He alone knows, visibly glorifying thee, [Name]!
*If thou celebratest with the Polyeleos: On "Lord, I have cried", Stichera on 6.*
*Glory... Tone 1:*
How should we not marvel at thy life? Or how should we not praise, O venerable [Name], thy life equal to the angels? A chaste mind, and the humble and quiet manner of thy meekness, and unflagging almsgiving? With all virtues thou wast adorned, O blessed one; therefore unutterable joy and the Kingdom of heaven await thee!
*Both now... Theotokion:*
*"The glory of all the world, who sprang forth from men..."*[^646]
*Entrance. Prokeimenon of the day, and three readings (see in the Service of a Prophet, fol. 1v).*[^647]
*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 2. Podoben: "House of Ephratha" (До́ме є҆ѵфра́ѳовъ):*[^648]
A house and dwelling-place of the All-Holy Spirit, O all-wise [Name], Thou wast; therefore make us also houses thereof, who honor thy holy memory!
*Verse: The righteous man shall rejoice in the Lord, and shall hope in Him.*[^649]

*Common Service to Fools for Christ's Sake*
Thy life shone forth like the sun in the hearts of the faithful, sending forth wonders; therefore illumine us also with never-waning light, who honor thine all-honorable memory!
*Verse: The righteous man shall flourish like a palm tree; like a cedar in Lebanon shall he be multiplied.*[^650]
Cease not praying, [Name], for thy household servants, whom by thy spiritual life thou didst enlighten: that all of us, rejoicing, may honor thy sacred solemnity!
*Glory... Tone 4:*
An earthly angel and a heavenly man wast thou shown to be, O most blessed one, a fountain of compunction, a flow of mercy, an abyss of wonders, a stream of healings, [Name], worthy of marvel! An olive tree of God truly fruitful, enlightening with the oil of thy labors those who faithfully praise thee, and entreating the Lover of mankind to deliver from perils those who with love honor thine all-honorable memory!
*Both now... Theotokion:*
*"Look down upon the entreaties of thy servants..."*[^651]
*If thou dost not celebrate, say this Theotokion:*
Deliver us from our afflictions, O Mother of Christ God, who didst give birth unto the Creator of all: that all of us may cry unto thee: Rejoice, sole protection of our souls!
*Stavrotheotokion. Podoben: "As one valiant among martyrs" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́блѧ):*[^652]
Beholding Christ crucified, the all-pure Mistress, Who puts to death the deceiver, weeping as the Sovereign Lady sang of the Compassionate One Who came forth from her; and marveling at His long-suffering, she cried aloud: O my most beloved Child, forget not Thy servants, tarry not, O Lover of mankind, my consolation!
*Troparion in the Typikon.*
*If there is no Typikon, say this Troparion, Tone 1:*[^653]
Having heard the voice of Thine Apostle Paul saying: "We are fools for Christ's sake" *Common Service to Fools for Christ's Sake*
...fools for Christ's sake; Thy servant, O Christ God, [Name], became a fool upon earth for Thy sake; therefore, honoring his memory, we entreat Thee, O Lord: save our souls!
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion according to the tone of the Troparion.*
*At Matins, at "God is the Lord", the same Troparion.*
*After the first Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 5. Podoben: "Co-beginningless Word" (Собезначáлное сло́во):*[^654]
As a firm diamond and an unshakable pillar against the assaults of the enemy, let us honor in songs the God-bearer, [Name]; for by patience he truly put to shame the adversary, and brought him to naught, and now prays for the salvation of our souls! *(Twice)*
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Awesome is the wonder of conception, and ineffable the manner of birthgiving recognized in thee, O pure Ever-Virgin; it astounds my mind and amazes my thought: thy glory, O Mother of God, is spread out unto all for the salvation of our souls!
*After the second Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 4. Podoben: "Speedily anticipate" (Ско́рѡ предварѝ):*[^655]
By fasting and self-restraint, and by lying upon the ground, thou didst wither carnal passions, and didst enlighten the soul; and in the heavens thou didst receive great recompense from Christ; therefore thou didst well-please God, and wast shown forth as a worthy vessel of wonders even after departure unto those running to the reliquary of thy relics, O most blessed [Name]: entreat Christ God to grant remission of sins unto those who honor thy holy memory with love! *(Twice)*
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Thou didst renew, O pure one, by thy divine birthgiving, the mortal essence of earthborn men that was corrupted by passions; and thou hast raised up all from death unto the life of incorruption: therefore as is due we all call thee blessed, O all-glorious Virgin, as thou didst foretell!

*Common Service to Fools for Christ's Sake*
*At the Polyeleos, Megalynarion:*
We call thee blessed, O holy righteous one, [Name], and we honor thy holy memory; for thou prayest for us unto Christ our God!
*Selected Psalm: Blessed is the man that fears the Lord...*[^656]
*After the Polyeleos, Sessional Hymn, Tone 4. Podoben: "Speedily anticipate" (Ско́рѡ предварѝ):*[^657]
Thou wast shown forth marvelous in thy patience, in every way pleasing Christ, O most blessed [Name]; with wise thought thou didst establish thy soul, enslaving carnal mindsets unto the Spirit, and choosing good things, to labor for God's sake, manly didst thou speak: "Though winter be burning cold, yet sweet is paradise; painful it is to labor, but blessed is the reception!" Therefore, O most blessed one, pray thou unto Christ God that our souls may be saved! *(Twice)*
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Thee we magnify, O Mother of God, crying aloud: Thou art alone blessed, for from thee ineffably was born Christ our God!
*Gradual, the first Antiphon of Tone 4.*
*Prokeimenon: The righteous man shall rejoice in the Lord, and shall hope in Him.*[^658]
*Verse: Hear my voice, O God, when I pray unto Thee.*[^659]
*Let every breath praise the Lord.*
*Gospel from Matthew, pericope 34.*[^660]
*After Psalm 50, Sticheron, Tone 8:*
O man of God, marvelous [Name], having ascended the ladder of virtues, whereby thou didst ascend unto the heavenly Jerusalem, and there beheld desired Christ: for Whose sake thou didst exhaust thy flesh, and thy mortal body *Common Service to Fools for Christ's Sake*
...thou didst exchange for immortal life. And unceasingly pray for us, that peace and great mercy may be granted unto our souls!
*If thou celebratest with the Polyeleos: Canon of the Mother of God on 4, and of the Fool-for-Christ on 8.*
*Tone 4, Ode 1. Heirmos:*
Having traversed the red deep of the sea with unwetted feet, ancient Israel of old, by the cross-like hands of Moses, conquered the power of Amalek in the wilderness.*[^661]
*Troparion 1:*
By communion with God, O venerable one, thou wast light, and having attained unto Him, O blessed [Name], by extreme desire, thou didst receive the fulfillment of thy good hopes, O father!
*Troparion 2:*
Enkindled with desire for God, thou didst forsake the world, having found Christ as thy fellow traveler, O glorious father [Name]: with Whom rejoicing thou didst traverse the saving path!
*Troparion 3:*
Desiring to be enriched with things in no way exhaustible, O wise one, thou didst valiantly cast away temporary wealth and glory, passing food, and worldly dignity!
*Theotokion:*
Knowing thee to be fair and chosen and all-honorable, O all-immaculate one, the Son of God became thy Son: make sons through grace, O Mother of God, of those who honor thee!
*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
Thy Church rejoices in Thee, O Christ, crying aloud: Thou art my strength, O Lord, and my refuge and confirmation!*[^662]
*Troparion 1:*
Diligently thou didst strip thyself for spiritual actions, conquering the enemy hosts with the help of the Holy Spirit!
*Troparion 2:*
By wounds and prayerful self-restraint, and vigils, O venerable father [Name], thou didst wound worldly passions!
*Troparion 3:*
Thy blessed mind put the enemy to death, [Name], having joyfully desired the mortification of Thy life-giving Master!

*Common Service to Fools for Christ's Sake*
*Theotokion:*
Him Who delivers men from the fierce fall of disobedience, the Cause of all, O pure and all-immaculate one, thou didst bear!
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 5. Podoben: "Co-beginningless Word" (Собезначáлное сло́во):*[^663]
Illumined by divine harmony, in the path of the trackless abyss thou didst walk faithfully unto divine radiances, O venerable father, and having fled the assaults of enemies, thou wast shown forth as a son of light and of day, [Name], divinely blessed!
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
All-pure Mother of God, city of Christians, deliver thy people who diligently call upon thee from dangers, and resist proud noetic enemies, that we may cry unto thee: Rejoice, blessed one!
*Stavrotheotokion:*
By the Cross of Thy Son, O woman full of the grace of God, all idolatrous deceit was abolished, and demonic power was trampled down; therefore we the faithful duly hymn thee ever and bless thee, and confessing thee truly as Mother of God, we magnify thee!
*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
Beholding Thee, the Sun of righteousness, lifted up upon the Cross, the Church stood in her place, worthily crying aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!*[^664]
*Troparion 1:*
Having united thyself unto the path of righteousness, thou didst enter into the God-protected divine city, O venerable [Name], enriched with saving radiance!
*Troparion 2:*
Single-minded, O venerable [Name], having seen God-bearing understanding, with divine decrees and with divine vesture thou wast clothed!
*Troparion 3:*
Finding thy heart a purified tablet, the grace of the Holy Spirit *Common Service to Fools for Christ's Sake*
...having found thy heart a purified tablet, the grace of the Holy Spirit, O father [Name], inscribed perfect dispassion, faith, and unhypocritical love!
*Theotokion:*
He Who sits upon the exalted cherubic throne, having thee as a throne, O Mary, Bride of God, rested upon thy hands, our all-glorified God!
*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
Thou, O my Lord, didst come into the world as Light, a holy Light turning from gloomy ignorance those who sing of Thee in faith!*[^665]
*Troparion 1:*
Having received a most radiant dawn, [Name], thou wast shown forth like the sun, and didst drive away demonic darkness, O worthy of glory!
*Troparion 2:*
By desire for Thee, O Christ, the all-venerable [Name] rejected carnal desire, shaking off worldly tow!
*Troparion 3:*
Having strengthened thy soul with divine power, thou didst take up thine abode in the world, putting away passions, O God-wise father, [Name]!
*Theotokion:*
Corrupt passion hath ceased, for the Virgin incorruptibly gave birth unto God the Word; beyond nature and speech she remains a Virgin!
*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
I will sacrifice unto Thee with the voice of praise, O Lord, the Church cries unto Thee, having been cleansed from the demonic blood by the blood that flowed through mercy from Thy side!*[^666]
*Troparion 1:*
Stretching forth all thy mind unto God with unceasing meditations, thou didst not spare carnal love, mediating for thyself adoption unto good things, [Name]!
*Troparion 2:*
Neither spot nor wrinkle didst thou receive, O blessed one, while living in the midst of the world; but rather by thine honorable entreaties thou cleansest from spiritual defilement those who run unto thee!
*Troparion 3:*
Having a mind higher than carnal reasonings, thou wast not wounded when naked among women; for with the garment of dispassion, [Name], thou wast clothed!
*Theotokion:*
With lips, mind, and heart, O all-pure Mother of God, we proclaim thee: through thee we have been reconciled unto God, who before were cast away by the transgression of our forefather!

*Common Service to Fools for Christ's Sake*
*Kontakion in the Typikon.*
*If there is no Typikon, say this Kontakion, Tone 8:*[^667]
Desiring the beauty on high, eagerly didst thou forsake lower carnal pleasures, by non-possessiveness of the worldly realm, passing through an angelic life; having died, O blessed [Name], with them unceasingly pray Christ God for all of us!
*Ikos:*
How shall I, a sinner, defiled in soul together with body, be able to search out thy life in the flesh, equal to the angels from on high, and thy marvelous wonders? For if many who are all-wise do not attain unto telling of thy great patience and humility, and fervent love toward Christ; yet trusting in thy guilelessness, O blessed one, thus I cry unto thee: Rejoice, most radiant star that didst shine forth from the orient of virtues, and illumined the universe with wonders! Rejoice, thou who didst hate parental love, and didst love with all thy heart Christ the only God Who is over all, and followed Him with irrevocable desire! Rejoice, for thou didst choose a perilous and foolish life according to the Apostle! Rejoice, thou who didst exchange corruptible and worthless things in hope of future blessings! Rejoice, thou who wast likened in patience unto ancient Job! Rejoice, passion-bearer of Christ unconquered, ever conquering the devil by thy humility! Rejoice, who like that ancient beggar Lazarus now restest in the bosom of Abraham! Rejoice, who after departure from this world art found a ready advocate *Common Service to Fools for Christ's Sake*
...and protector for those in perils and temptations! Rejoice, thou who swiftly anticipatest those who invoke thee everywhere! Rejoice, God-wise [Name]!
*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
In the Persian furnace the Abrahamite children, enkindled rather by love of piety than by flame, cried out: Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy glory, O Lord!*[^668]
*Troparion 1:*
Possessed, O father [Name], by Christ-like divine love to save others, joyfully, crying aloud, thou didst give over thine entire self: Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy glory, O Lord!
*Troparion 2:*
Having received food given unto thee from heaven, O father [Name], therewith thou didst nourish those who obediently received thy commands and forsook downward-dragging sin, O blessed one!
*Troparion 3:*
Having followed patristic traditions, O wise father [Name], thou didst command those crying aloud: "Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy glory, O Lord!" to flee the pernicious impieties of Origen![^669]
*Theotokion:*
Rejoice, sanctified divine dwelling-place of the Most High! For through thee joy was given, O Mother of God, unto those who cry: Blessed art thou among women, O all-immaculate Sovereign Lady!
*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
Stretching out his hands, Daniel stopped the mouths of the lions in the pit, and the young men, lovers of piety, girded about with virtue, quenched the power of fire, crying aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!*[^670]
*Troparion 1:*
An all-radiant star wast thou, leading unto life those that are in the mire of passions: for thou makest unclean women to live chastely, and drivest away fierce diseases, crying aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!
*Troparion 2:*
Being sound in mind in the concealment of wise wonders, O venerable one, thou didst catch many to play the fool for the Lord's sake, knowing *Common Service to Fools for Christ's Sake*
...how to be mocked, crying with a purest soul, [Name]: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!
*Troparion 3:*
The Lord alone is perfect, Who glorifies His own worthily, Who at thy carrying forth and sending unto the grave, O blessed one, honors thee with angelic glorious praises, as thou criest: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!
*Theotokion:*
The Lord Who delivers us shone forth from thee, O pure Virgin: Whom entreat, O Sovereign Lady, to enlighten those who hymn thee, and to deliver us from afflictions, who cry aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!
*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
A cornerstone was cut without hands from the uncut mountain, from thee, O Virgin, Christ, Who united separated natures: therefore rejoicing, we magnify thee, O Mother of God!*[^671]
*Troparion 1:*
With all thy heart, [Name], having eagerly offered thyself unto the Trinity, and having virtuously received rays therefrom, thou wast light-like, and dancest with the angels!
*Troparion 2:*
Having been united by divine desire, and enlightened by self-restraint, already wast thou in communion and enjoyment of future blessings, [Name], divinely blessed!
*Troparion 3:*
By thy sufferings and sweats thou didst receive joy having no end: nourished wherein, [Name], most blessed one, remember us unceasingly!
*Theotokion:*
Save me, O thou who didst give birth unto the Deliverer and Benefactor of all; scatter the clouds of my soul, O holy Sovereign Lady, Cloud of Light, and make me strong against the passions that war against me!
*Exapostilarion:*[^672]
Having ever removed thyself from worldly cohabitation, with a blameless life thou didst well-please Christ our God Who is over all: Whom now pray thou for us thy servants, who faithfully celebrate *Common Service to Fools for Christ's Sake*[^673]
...who faithfully celebrate thy most honorable memory, O divinely wise [Name].
*Theotokion:*
O Virgin who didst give birth unto the Hypostatic Wisdom, the Supersubstantial Word, and the Physician of all: heal the wounds and sores of my soul, grievous and long-enduring, and calm the passionate musings of my heart.
*If thou celebratest with Polyeleos: At the Praises, Stichera, Tone 4.*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Thou hast given a sign" (Да́лъ є҆сѝ зна́менїе):*
The all-good Lord hath given the righteous [Name] as an example of endurance and courage, and one most marvelous, shining with virtues, words, and deeds, adorned with chastity and meekness. Therefore we glorify Thine ineffable dispensation, O all-powerful Jesus and Savior of our souls. *(Twice)*
*Sticheron 2:*
Righteous, guileless, true, blameless, and faithful, turning away from every evil thing, didst thou appear, O blessed [Name], enlightened by virtues and shining with piety. Therefore we call thee blessed, and celebrate today thy holy and honorable falling asleep, rejoicing, O divinely wise one.
*Sticheron 3:*
Arrayed with chastity and true faith as with a costly purple robe, [Name], and crowned with righteousness and meekness as with a divine diadem, thou didst desire to reign with those who suffer for the sake of Christ. And now thou standest before the King of hosts, the all-merciful Jesus and Savior of our souls.
*Glory... Tone 8:*
The accomplishments of thy virtues, O divinely wise [Name], struck awe into the hearts of the faithful: for who that hears of thine immeasurable humility and patience will not marvel? Thy meekness and guilelessness toward all, compassion toward the afflicted, and *Common Service to Fools for Christ's Sake*
speedy help unto those in distress; a storm-free harbor for those who sail, and good prosperity for those who travel: in a manner fitting God thou didst anticipate all things, O worthy of wonder! And now thou hast been crowned with an unfading crown from the hand of God the Almighty: Whom pray thou to save our souls.
*Both now... Theotokion:*
Accept, O Sovereign Lady, the prayers of thy servants, and deliver us from every distress and sorrow.
*At the Liturgy, Beatitudes from the Canon, Ode 3 and Ode 6.*
*Prokeimenon, Tone 7:*
Honorable in the sight of the Lord is the death of His venerable ones.
*Verse: What shall I render unto the Lord for all that He hath rendered unto me?*
*Epistle to the Corinthians, Pericope 131.*[^674]
